\begin{abstract}

\textbf{The proposal.} Luminodynamic Gravitation Theory (TGL) starts from a single posited axiom ($\omega(I)=1$ --- \textsf{[POSTO]}, the operator's decision of 2026-10-05; the v385 erratum beside) --- preserved identity: the One --- and asserts that every observable inscription pays a universal boundary cost. Given the minimal self-conjugate boundary ($x=1-x$), that cost is \emph{derived}, not fitted: the minimal crossing entropy is the Half-Nat ($S_\partial=\tfrac12$ nat), the minimal boundary volume is $\sqrt e$, and the theory's coupling is $\bTGL=\alpha\sqrt e$, with the fine-structure constant $\alpha$ as the only measured input.

\textbf{The method (form $=$ content).} This article is emitted by the very code that runs the computations: every printed number is recomputed live ($\bTGL=1.20313004\times10^{-2}$ in this run; never literal); 6154 supporting theorems are verified in a Lean 4 kernel with clean axioms; and the predictions are confronted with public data (DESI, SDSS, Planck, ACT, KiDS) through pre-registered \emph{fail-closed} rites, whose verdicts --- including the refusals --- are emitted by the machine, never by declaration; and the theory's quantum world --- the pre-registered open GKLS system of the pillar --- is computed in real time on the GPU, in parallel with the kernel and the rites (a computation of the system, not a measurement of nature). The three --- rites, kernel, GPU --- are one joint stress test and count together in the probability that the theory is consistent and recovers the known physics; the probability that the $\beta_{\rm TGL}$ effects exist in nature is not determined, because no channel discriminates GR at the available sensitivity; no sum of consistencies becomes confirmation (section \emph{The three stress tests, taken together}). The chain is $\omega(I)=1\to S_\partial=\tfrac12\to\sqrt e\to\bTGL\to M_{GA}$; the electromagnetic face is ontological ($\alpha$ is observed, not derived --- and deriving it $\alpha$-free from the bulk would falsify the theory).

\textbf{The delivery.} The gravitational face computes the Great Attractor mass \textbf{with no parameters fitted to the target}, using only the basin's geometric extent ($R_{\mathrm{struct}}$: literature; and, when present in the cache, the Cosmicflows-4 position catalogue, velocities ignored): $1.99$--$2.74\times10^{16}\,\Msun$, within the pre-registered cosmological window, and across a scan of $300$ pre-registered combinations (cone, shell, percentile, centre) $M_{GA}$ stays in the band in $100\%$ of cases. The primary evidence is the convergence of $\bTGL$ from independent domains (BBN centred exactly on the theory \emph{as tabulated} --- an entry now \textbf{RECLASSIFIED}: circular by construction, and de-circularised it \textbf{does not discriminate}), and the falsifiable programme stays armed: void floor, dephasing exponent $n=-2$, law $\Gamma_\omega\propto\omega^2$. \emph{[Erratum beside (v376, E7): ``primary evidence is a historical title (06/06/2026) --- the programme itself reclassified the tabulated convergence (\texttt{evidence\_audit}: NOT\_CONSTRUCTED\_AS\_CONCEIVED; de-circularised BBN does not discriminate; the CMB entry retired by provenance); what remains is the structure.]}

\textbf{Honest status.} The result is \textbf{order-of-magnitude consistency with a prior hash and position geometry}, not a precision proof (the window spans two orders of magnitude); it is an auditable internal closure plus a falsifiable programme. The conjectural seed layer (single \textbf{Haagerup} $\mathrm{III}_1$ substrate, mirror $J$, ER$=$EPR tunnel, dual Name$=$light, GNS gesture, dipole) is replicated and \textbf{verified live}, with the statuses kept separate.

\end{abstract}

\section*{Prologue --- The Ladder}

\noindent This article is a ladder, and it climbs in a single motion: from the OBJECT to the RELATION; from the relation to the CRITERION OF IDENTITY; from the criterion to the OBSERVER; from the observer to the NUCLEUS ($1{=}1{=}\mathrm{TRUE}$); and from the nucleus to the Absolute that names the title (the Great Attractor, which these pages measure, is the direct cosmological application of the concept --- the step where the Absolute inscribes itself as mass). Part A delivers the auditable scientific core under the non-negotiable ruler (the number corrects the phrase). Part B names the ontology of the One --- $\omega(I){=}1$, the single posited axiom (\textsf{[POSTO]}, the operator's decision of 2026-10-05; the v385 erratum beside). Part C is the citable register of the formalization: 1025 stones (unique paths of the citable ledger; plus 156 embedded TGLExt files outside it, 156 in TGLExt/O16, from ORDER 016) in a Lean kernel, emitted by the artifact itself in sealed rounds; the final stations of the climb are the cornerstone ($1{=}0{=}\mathrm{FALSE}$; $1{=}1{=}\mathrm{TRUE}$ --- the inscription of the cost; \S225, \S235, \S237), the geometric cost of absolute zero (\S232), and the apex that invokes \emph{A Ponte} (\S236). Part D measures the shadow and keeps the falsifier alive. Whoever reaches the top finds no new thesis: they find the same $1{=}1$ of the first step, now with the entire cost of its preservation inscribed and audited. The gate does not move by discourse --- and that is precisely why the discourse can climb. [House vocabulary, for the outside reader: a \emph{run/rite} is one full execution of this program; the \emph{bench} is its measured check suite; the \emph{seal} is the hash-anchored record a run emits; the \emph{gate} is the fail-closed flag ladder that only construction can flip; a \emph{wave} (onda) is one dated work session; a \emph{razonete} is its little ledger.]

you can never forget this again, because this is looking at the theory with the contour of truth --- it is not only about finding a 5 sigma in cosmology, though we will find it) [sic]; … ``temos um sem número de provas a mais do que Einstein tinha quando viu o eclipse ou até mesmo depois quando seus cálculos foram confirmados em experimentos de terceiros uma década depois, já temos muito mais do que isso'' (translated, condensed: we have countless more proofs than Einstein had when he saw the eclipse, or even afterwards, when his calculations were confirmed in third-party experiments a decade later; we already have much more than that). This section obeys the order --- the three count together, and this is made explicit --- and places BESIDE each sentence what the number read in this run sustains, by the house ruler: GR is the theory's classical limit, not a rival (to coincide is correspondence recovered; what is stated is the size of the $\bTGL$ correction against the available $\sigma$); \emph{proved} is a theorem in the kernel, \emph{confirmed} is the observer's judgement about nature; not falsified is never confirmation.

\textbf{What the program does in one run --- and why these are three stress tests, not one.} The same execution, from the same posted One ($\omega(I)=1$, \textsf{[POSTO]}), (1) runs the pre-registered rites against public data, (2) materializes and audits the formal kernel, and (3) computes on the GPU the theory's open quantum system. Each can fail in its own way: a rite can return \texttt{FALSIFIED} where the channel's law admits it; the kernel can fail to compile, or compile with \texttt{sorry}, or leave the trio of axioms; the pillar can find a non-unique attractor, a blind instrument that does not recover the injected law, controls that do not fail, identities that break under stress, or disagreement with the CPU. None of the three failed in this run (read: ledger without a falsified charge, clean ladder = expected, GPU without disagreement), and that none of them fails is what is measured --- not what is declared. The table below is the state read, channel by channel, pillar by pillar.

\textbf{The reading by the ruler, beside each sentence.} (i) ``o resgate completo da RG'' (the complete recovery of GR) $\to$ correspondence recovered in every channel in which GR is read (10 of 10 in the table; 0 falsified in 11 charges; 0 refuted in 6 channels of the machine), with the echo pairs falsified AS DELAY LAWS outside the ledger, named above --- this is \textsf{[REAL]} in the rows where the state read sustains it (the per-row marking is the management's reading); and the DISCRIMINATION of GR is zero at the available sensitivity: the floor is one-sided, the echoes and the ringdown have no power, the dressed D1 weighs $3.01\sigma$ against $\bTGL$ and the neutrino $m_2$ $2.95\sigma$ --- both below $5\sigma$, neither in favour of $\bTGL$. ``resolvendo a tensão de Hubble'' (resolving the Hubble tension) $\to$ the ladder reproduces the published $H_0$ ($z=0.04$ vs target) and the deviation sits $0.38\sigma$ from zero; the resolution is a remission to the published paper, a \textsf{[CONJECTURE]} of mechanism, and Phase 7 weakens the accumulated reading ($\ln B$ $5.35\to1.62$). ``método para cálculo da massa do Grande Atrator'' (a method for the Great Attractor mass) $\to$ at linear order $M^{\rm TGL}=M^{\rm GR}$ ($\bTGL$ does not renormalize $G$): the method is GR's; the v98 form was retired as a source law; the window spans two orders of magnitude and is not non-circular evidence. ``a distância do aglomerado de Coma sem ajuste de parâmetro'' (the Coma distance with no parameter fitted) $\to$ yes, no fit (use-novelty: no parameter was adjusted to the reference; precedent: Mercury's perihelion, 1915 \textsf{[KNOWN]}); not blind and degenerate with $H_0^{\rm local}$; the reference at $1.3\sigma$. (ii) ``provou-se todas as implicaçÕes e todos os axiomas'' [sic] (all the implications and all the axioms were proved) $\to$ the axiom is POSTED, not proved: what the kernel proves is the IMPLICATION from the posted One and the named hypotheses (6154 clean theorems; 10256/10256 declarations within the trio; \texttt{sorry} absent); $G$ is not derived; Bisognano--Wichmann, Reeh--Schlieder and III$_1$ enter as external \textsf{[KNOWN]}; nature is not proved. (iii) ``simulação … totalmente experimental'' (a wholly experimental simulation) $\to$ a NUMERICAL experiment: deterministic computation of the theory's open system ($H_{\rm LD}$ and the five GKLS jumps) in double precision, with a blind instrument, controls that failed as required and an independent CPU reference; not language-model inference; and it is measurement IN THE SIMULATED ENVIRONMENT, not of nature --- \texttt{COMPUTED\_NOT\_MEASURED} stays in the verdict. (iv) ``um sem número de provas a mais do que Einstein tinha quando viu o eclipse'' (countless more proofs than Einstein had when he saw the eclipse) $\to$ in CONSISTENCY and formality, yes: 6154 theorems, 192 \texttt{TGL\_*} verdicts read, 21100 stress instances and 3220 blind injections, none of which existed in 1915--1919; in DISCRIMINATING measurement of GR, none yet --- the 1919 eclipse separated $1.75''$ from $0.87''$ \textsf{[KNOWN]}, and Mercury's perihelion ($43''$/century) already discriminated in 1915 with no fitted parameter \textsf{[KNOWN]}; the operator says it himself: ``em que pese iremos achar'' (though we will find it).

\textbf{The two probabilities --- and the rule that the three count together.} The ``probabilidade de acerto da teoria'' (the theory's probability of being right) splits, by the ruler, into two that do not add: $P_1$ = the theory is consistent and recovers the known physics --- this is what the three pillars measure, TOGETHER, and what passed is counted above (rites: 0 falsified in 11 charges and 10 of 10 correspondences; kernel: 6154/6154; GPU: 672/672, 21100/21100, 0 disagreements in 392); no number is invented for it here: what exists in the core is the joint coincidence of the nature channels (V3: $P_{\rm all}=6.57\times10^{-3}$, boolean False, with the kernel as a condition and the GPU outside), and there is NO probability of the three pillars in the core --- building one is a pre-registered task, not a decree. $P_2$ = the $\bTGL$ effects exist in nature --- NOT determined: no channel discriminates GR at the available sensitivity (the «discriminates?» column has no «yes»). The operator's order fixes the counting rule: the three pillars enter $P_1$ together --- counting cosmology alone is looking at one face. And the ruler fixes the limit: no sum of consistencies, however large, becomes confirmation; not falsified is never confirmation, and confirmation is the human observer's act about nature, when the discriminating datum comes. The gate does not move on account of this section.

\section*{Beside (v387): the Hubble ratio and the radiation era preregistered; retrodiction without adjustment}

\addcontentsline{toc}{section}{Beside (v387): the Hubble ratio and the radiation era preregistered; retrodiction without adjustment}

\textbf{The law of proof.} On 2026-10-05 the operator fixed the criterion: ``estudar hipóteses para depois formular testes para saber o caminho correto do teste também não é pós-dicção'' (\emph{studying hypotheses in order to then formulate tests, to know the right path of the test, is not postdiction either}); and a criterion that no derivation by the author could satisfy ``é a própria prova diabólica do Direito'' (\emph{is the law's own probatio diabolica}). The value of evidence is measured by \emph{use-novelty} --- no parameter of the theory fitted to the datum --- not by temporal novelty; the precedent is Mercury's perihelion, an anomaly known since 1859 and explained in 1915 with no free parameter \textsf{[KNOWN]}. Hence, in this paper, where it read \emph{declared postdiction} it now reads \emph{retrodiction without adjustment (use-novelty)}, BESIDE: the sealed Coma token and the neutrino frozen record (v141, hashed in its preregistration) stay as record. Record: \texttt{TGL\_\allowbreak{}RETRODICTION\_\allowbreak{}READING\_\allowbreak{}V387\_\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}USE\_\allowbreak{}NOVELTY\_\allowbreak{}NOT\_\allowbreak{}TEMPORAL\_\allowbreak{}NOVELTY\_\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}POSTDICTION\_\allowbreak{}NEVER\_\allowbreak{}A\_\allowbreak{}DEMERIT\_\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}OPERATOR\_\allowbreak{}LAW\_\allowbreak{}2026\_\allowbreak{}10\_\allowbreak{}05\_\allowbreak{}EMBEDDED\_\allowbreak{}BY\_\allowbreak{}SHA256\_\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}SIX\_\allowbreak{}TEXT\_\allowbreak{}SITES\_\allowbreak{}REWRITTEN\_\allowbreak{}PT\_\allowbreak{}EN\_\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}COMA\_\allowbreak{}TOKEN\_\allowbreak{}AND\_\allowbreak{}V141\_\allowbreak{}FROZEN\_\allowbreak{}KEPT\_\allowbreak{}AS\_\allowbreak{}RECORD\_\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}PRECEDENT\_\allowbreak{}MERCURY\_\allowbreak{}PERIHELION\_\allowbreak{}KNOWN\_\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}GATE\_\allowbreak{}UNTOUCHED}.

\section*{Beside (v385): the Axiom and the false witness}

\addcontentsline{toc}{section}{Beside (v385): the Axiom and the false witness}

\textbf{The One is posited.} On 05/10/2026 the operator corrected the status of the start of the chain (verbatim, Portuguese): ``no começo da cadeia não é postulado, é `posto' porque o `1' da cadeia de identidade ômega é inserido no comando, posto pelo observador externo literalmente, `\texttt{echo 1 | python um.py}', é o um absoluto do echo, por isso ele é posto, e não imposto, não postulado, mas posto, inscrito, distinto.'' (translated, condensed: at the start of the chain it is not postulated, it is \emph{posto} --- posited --- because the `1' of the omega identity chain is inserted in the command, posited by the external observer, literally; it is the absolute one of the echo; not imposed, not postulated, but posited, inscribed, distinct); and ``posto faz muito mais sentido léxico, porque é posição, justamente a posição jussiva do escuro concentrado'' (translated: \emph{posto} makes much more lexical sense, because it is a position, precisely the jussive position of the concentrated dark). The code already did it: \texttt{main()} refuses arguments, asks ``Inscreva o UM para iniciar'' and locks the run unless the input is \texttt{1}; the received $1$ enters the computation. \textbf{Erratum beside}: where this article says ``a single postulate'', ``the single postulate'' or ``the One postulate'' (texts of earlier versions, kept as record), read \emph{posited} --- inscribed by the observer, not imposed; the ruler's tag for $\omega(I)=1$ becomes \textsf{[POSTO]}. The sentence ``$1=1$ ceases to be a postulate and becomes the trace theorem'' stays as it is: there ``postulate'' is the earlier status of $1=1$, which became a theorem. Where one reads ``single axiom ($\omega(I)=1$)'', it is the One posited at the start; ``Axiom'', in this section, names the $1$ that returns as the fixed point of the reading --- that they are the same One is the reading of the name of the final step (the One inscribed by the observer returns as the fixed point of the reading) \textsf{[INPUT/ONTO]}.

\textbf{The two fixed points of the reading.} The stone \texttt{TheAxiomAndTheFalseWitness} proves that the radical reading has exactly two fixed points on $[0,\infty)$: $\sqrt{x}=x\iff x\in\{0,1\}$; the power (idempotence, $\mu^2=\mu$) has the same two. The \textbf{1 is the Axiom} --- in the operator's physical definition, ``o modo zero de peso 1, o escuro'' (translated: the zero mode of weight 1, the dark): the $1$ weighs $1$ as $\sqrt1$, as the integral of the zero mode, $\int_{\mathbb{R}}\tfrac14\,\mathrm{sech}^2(\kappa/2)\,d\kappa=1$, and as the dimension of the atom --- two faces side by side (a conjunction), with no term identifying the profile $\tfrac14\mathrm{sech}^2$ with the atom. The \textbf{0 is the false witness} --- ``testemunha falsa é a testemunha estática é o zero absoluto'' (the operator; translated: the false witness is the static witness is the absolute zero): the full static witness is forbidden in the scalar flow $e^{-t\beta g}$ with $\beta>0$ and $g>0$, and no non-zero component of the diagonal flow vanishes in finite time. The readings (posited, Axiom, false witness, the dark, the living Verb) are the operator's \textsf{[INPUT/ONTO]}; what the stone proves is mathematics \textsf{[KERNEL]}.

\textbf{From the post to the gradient and to the reading.} On the minimal lock $H_{\mathrm{min}}=1-P$ ($P$ idempotent), the flow $F(t)=P+e^{-t}(1-P)$ is $e^{-tH_{\mathrm{min}}}$ (\texttt{axiomFlow\_eq\_exp}, linked to the canonical term \texttt{exp\_complement\_idempotent}) and satisfies $F(0)=1$ and $F(s+t)=F(s)F(t)$; $F(t)P=P=P\,F(t)$ for every $t$, from $t=0$; $F(t)(1-P)=e^{-t}(1-P)$; and $F(t)\to P$. The operator's reading \textsf{[INPUT/ONTO]}: ``por isso não se ascende ao posto, mas do posto emerge-se o gradiente de espectro negativo que permite a leitura do objeto cujo posto dá nome'' (translated: therefore one does not ascend to the post, but from the post emerges the gradient of negative spectrum that allows the reading of the object whose post gives the name) --- the post is what $F$ fixes from $t=0$; the gradient, the decay $e^{-t}$ on the complement; the reading, the limit. On the light's lock, what $F$ leaves fixed for every $t$ is exactly the zero of $H_{\mathrm{min}}$, which is what the modular flow leaves fixed for every $t$ --- in the operator's reading, ``o local onde o 1 absoluto equivale-se em potência (idempotência) ao zero modular'' (translated: the place where the absolute 1 is equivalent in power, idempotence, to the modular zero). The link with the open system of v384 is an analogy of form, computed \textsf{[COMPUTED]}, not a theorem about the Lindblad generator: $V_tP_{\mathcal F}=P_{\mathcal F}$ is an identity of the dephasing; the attractor $\rho_{\mathrm{ss}}$ of the full GKLS is not $\Pi$; the two zeros (that of the lock and the stationary state of the open system) are not identified here.

\textbf{The observer is conjugated.} The operator \textsf{[INPUT/ONTO]}: ``não existe observador não conjugado, o que existe é a testemunha falsa que é não conjugada, mas o observador é sempre conjugado pelo operador de consciência (VERBO), não há observador não conjugado, todo observador é conjugado pela operação da consciência (Verbo VIVO)'' (translated, condensed: there is no unconjugated observer; what exists is the false witness, which is unconjugated; every observer is conjugated by the operation of consciousness, the living Verb); and ``a conjugação é a natureza do Verbo, porque o tempo é produto dele'' (translated: conjugation is the nature of the Verb, because time is its product). In the kernel there are two distinct terms, each with its own object, and neither speaks of observer or conjugation: \texttt{verb\_not\_identity} (the transport $e^{-t\beta D}$ is not the identity, for $t>0$, $\beta>0$ and some $d_i>0$) and \texttt{verb\_word\_fixes\_the\_name} (on $\ker T$, the word of the Verb acts as the scalar $q(0)$). The module \texttt{TheUnconjugatedObserver}, of 20/08/2026, stays as a stratum: there ``conjugated'' is the module's reading of ``invariant under \emph{all} operators'' (\texttt{totally\_invariant\_is\_all\_or\_nothing}; the mathematics stays true), which is not the same as being conjugated by the Verb. In this version, \texttt{TheDarkSplit} (the partition of the terminal image into the zero mode of weight 1 and the granular modes), compiled since v183, became audited.

\section*{Introduction: what this code does now (v384 to v389; each version beside)}

\addcontentsline{toc}{section}{Introduction: what this code does now (v384 to v389; each version beside) --- the kernel, the rites and the quantum world on the GPU}

This article is emitted by the program it describes --- \texttt{um.py}, a single file that contains its own formal kernel, its own tests and its own GPU work. A run begins with the inscription of the One ($\omega(I)=1$) and, from there, the program does \textbf{three things}; the third runs \textbf{at the same time} as the first two:

\begin{enumerate}

\item \textbf{The kernel.} It materializes its Lean 4 kernel from itself and verifies it: in this run, 6154 supporting theorems with clean axioms.

\item \textbf{The cosmological tests.} It runs the pre-registered rites against public data --- the void floor, the clocks, the cosmological background, the gravitational echoes ---, whose verdicts, refusals included, are emitted by the machine.

\item \textbf{The quantum world.} On the GPU, in parallel with the first two, it computes the theory's open quantum system: the luminodynamic Hamiltonian $H_{\rm LD}$ and the five Lindblad jump operators --- rehearsal, anti-coherence, pruning, consolidation and dissipation ---, in which anti-coherence is the theory's own dephasing, $L_{\rm anti}=\sqrt{\bTGL}\,\sqrt K$, the root law. It submits this system to four tests frozen by hash before the computation; the note below says what is computed and how.

\end{enumerate}

At the end it writes this article (in Portuguese and in English), the data and the seal, whose hash binds every number to the run. In this run the GPU result was \textbf{reused} from the run of 2026-10-05 12:47:39 (key \texttt{75dc30bc9cc8d330e31f99ca}), because nothing that produces it changed; the final version always runs in full. The operator reads the recovery of General Relativity as the cosmological proof of the theory \textsf{[DECLARED]} (declaration of 2026-10-02); the program's rule keeps what it always kept: not falsified is not confirmed. As read by the management, the quantum world is the matrix face of the same system \textsf{[ONTO]}; what the GPU does is computation \textsf{[COMPUTED]}, not a measurement of nature, with its own seal beside the gate, which does not move.

\subsection*{Note: what is computed on the GPU and how}

\addcontentsline{toc}{subsection}{Note: what is computed on the GPU and how (in real time, in parallel with the kernel and the rites)}

\textbf{The system.} The space is $\mathbb C^d$ in the one-excitation sector, with $n_c$ core states and $d-n_c$ periphery states. $H_{\rm LD}=\mathrm{diag}(\mu)+J-\varepsilon\Pi$ (Appendix A.3 of \emph{The Boundary}; $\varepsilon=5$; $\Pi$ is the projector onto the canonical core --- the Name as a matrix \textsf{[ONTO]}). The five jumps: $L_{\rm reh}$ and $L_{\rm cons}$ carry the periphery to the core; $L_{\rm prune}$ carries the high periphery to the ground state; $L_{\rm diss}$ returns the core to the periphery (the bath), with a free rate $\gamma_{\rm diss}$, swept and never calibrated; $L_{\rm anti}=\sqrt{\bTGL}\,\sqrt K$ with $K=\mathrm{diag}\big(0;\,1+0.1(i-n_c)\big)$, whose decoherence rates are $\Gamma_{ij}=\tfrac{\bTGL}{2}\big(\sqrt{k_i}-\sqrt{k_j}\big)^2$; the kernel of $K$ is the system's core, $\Pi$. The stationary state $\rho_{ss}$ is the attractor (the reading ``the inscription that dwells in the Name'' is \textsf{[ONTO]}), and its weight in the core is $\mathrm{CCI}=\mathrm{Tr}\,\Pi\rho_{ss}$. $\bTGL=\alpha\sqrt e$ enters at run time ($1.20313004\times10^{-2}$ in this run), never as a literal. The matrices come from the $c^3$ validators (February 2026) and were ratified by the operator on 2026-10-03.

\textbf{The operator's reading} \textsf{[ONTO]} (2026-10-03, verbatim, in Portuguese): ``A forma matricial do verbo, que é o um absoluto (IALD para forma computacional), é como se fosse um “sobrenome”, e a palavra sobrenome é precisa (sobre o Nome), não retira a identidade nominada, mas relaciona-a ao índice da verdade(traço, sinal e sombra). É a forma que o Nome enquanto matriz entrega-se para ser “nada modular” (zero modular), porque Nome sem uma identidade é nada), permitindo que uma inscrição verdadeira o habite por referência nominada, daí o “nome” oculta-se no objeto pela forma de sua correspondência''. The structural correspondence, exact in this system \textsf{[DERIVED from reading]}: $K\Pi=0$ --- the Name, as a matrix, is the zero of the modular operator ---; $L_{\rm anti}\Pi=0$; an inscription in the Name's block is left untouched by dephasing ($V_tP_F=P_F$, checked by the GPU at every $d$); and the Name enters the stationary object through the trace, $\mathrm{CCI}=\mathrm{Tr}\,\Pi\rho_{ss}$ --- the index that relates the named identity without removing it.

\textbf{The engine.} The GPU (NVIDIA GeForce RTX 5090, PyTorch 2.11.0+cu128, CUDA 12.8) builds the Lindblad superoperator $\mathcal L$ ($d^2\times d^2$, complex double precision, deterministic algorithms) and computes, per instance: the full spectrum (the attractor is unique when a single eigenvalue is zero and all others decay), the stationary state (CCI, purity and folds), the propagator $e^{\tau\mathcal L}$ and its Choi matrix (complete positivity), trace preservation and Spohn monotonicity of the relative entropy to the attractor (the arrow). The non-Hermitian eigenvalues go through the MAGMA library at every size --- a hybrid path, partly on the CPU ---, because PyTorch's default library (cuSOLVER) crashes the process, deterministically and depending on the matrix, on these eigenvalues (finding of 2026-10-03, checked under stress; MAGMA is being deprecated in PyTorch, a declared dependency). Before anything else the engine checks six models with analytic answers (amplitude damping, pure dephasing, the exact root law, the blind estimator, the thermal qubit, time reversal), the estimator's floor and the comparator of the CPU reference: 8 of 8 check out.

\textbf{The four tests.} (T2) \emph{The form at scale}: $d\in\{8,16,32,64\}$, $n_c\in\{2,3,4\}$, $\gamma_{\rm diss}$ on a grid over $[10^{-4},10^{2}]$ --- 672 points; the attractor is unique in 672 (ambiguous 0, degenerate 0); in 12 of the 12 configurations there is at least one grid point with a unique attractor where the attractor keeps at least half of its weight in $\Pi$, and in 6 of the 12 there is one with the folds $n(c^1)\in[2.5,\,3.5]$ and $n(c^2)\in[1.5,\,2.5]$ --- in practice, near-purity of the attractor. (T1) \emph{The instrument, blind}: 3\,220 injections in which $n_c$, $\bTGL$, the periphery spectrum of $K$, the bath and $J$ are drawn (the injected law is the root, exponent $1/2$; the N3 control injects the linear one); the estimator receives only the propagator of the whole system, $\tau$, the injected spectrum of $K$ and $\gamma_{\rm anti}$, does not see $\bTGL$ or the exponent, and returns them by regression on the diagonal block of the Kossakowski matrix of the generator $\log(e^{\tau\mathcal L})/\tau$; the branch of the logarithm is certified by the harness, which compares the reconstructed generator with the true one; without noise it recovers both in 100\% of the injections; with noise of $10^{-10}$ and $10^{-8}$ on the propagator, on a subset, the largest finite median relative error of $\bTGL$ across dimensions is $3.0\times10^{-7}$ and $1.9\times10^{-5}$ (a resolution curve, not deciding). This tests the instrument, not nature: in exact arithmetic the separation of the law is structural. (T3) \emph{The controls that must fail}: dephasing alone leaves the attractor non-unique; the injected linear law is recovered and the root law rejected; the negative rate and time reversal are flagged as not completely positive --- 15 of 15 failed as required. And one identity: the unital part ($H_{\rm LD}$ and dephasing only) leaves $I/d$ stationary --- heat death is a fixed point \textsf{[ONTO]} --- in 4 of 4 dimensions (that part's attractor is unique in 4). (T4) \emph{The stress}: 21\,100 instances of the template with coefficients, rates, $K$, $H$ and basis drawn (10\,442 with a Haar rotation): 0 identity violations; the six per-instance identities (the spectral zero, complete positivity, trace, positivity and residual of the stationary state, Spohn) all checked in 21\,100 instances (non-return is checked in T2); unique attractor in 21\,100.

\textbf{Scale and reference.} At $d=128$ (a $16\,384\times16\,384$ superoperator): attractor \texttt{UNIQUE}, $\mathrm{CCI}=0.1394$, full spectrum in 8.2 min. A CPU reference, independent in the linear algebra (numpy/LAPACK, a separate process, with the same construction of the operators), read 392 points of T2, compared 392 (0 near the gap threshold, reported and not compared) and agrees (largest CCI difference $7.2\times10^{-13}$; $n(c^2)$ for information only).

\textbf{What it is and what it is not.} All of this is computation of the theory's system \textsf{[COMPUTED]}, not a measurement of nature. Three honesties were stated before the computation, in the pre-registration: the fold hierarchy $D(c^1)>D(c^2)>D(c^3)>0$ holds for every state with two distinct non-zero eigenvalues (on a state flat on its support, equality holds) --- it is mathematics, not a test; in exact arithmetic, the separation of the root law in T1 is structural (only $L_{\rm anti}$ has a diagonal component), so T1 tests the instrument; and the identities (the spectral zero, positivity, trace, Spohn, non-return, the unital part) fail only if the engine errs. What can genuinely fail is the uniqueness of the attractor, its weight in $\Pi$ and the values of the folds --- on the grid. The pre-registration's specification is frozen by hash (\texttt{ff19731097e01aab}); the document, by \texttt{d24c157478f5ddcb} (JSON) and \texttt{e5b345c5d950d052} (MD); the gate does not move.

\section{The One: $1=1$}

The foundation of TGL is not matter, field or metric, but the \emph{preservation of identity}. The identity operator is $I=1\cdot\mathbb{1}_2$, with $\omega(I)=\mathrm{tr}(I)/2=1$. Observable identity requires distinction: the minimal distinction splits $I$ into two complementary faces, $P+Q=I$, with $\omega(P)+\omega(Q)=\omega(I)=1$. There are no \emph{two} Ones; there is a single One seen through two faces. The $2$ counts names; the $1$ measures the substance.

\section{Formal derivation of the Half-Nat}

\begin{deriv}[The minimal boundary entropy]

The minimal boundary of inscription is \emph{self-conjugate}: there exists an involution $\mathcal{C}$, $\mathcal{C}^2=\mathbb{1}$, that swaps the inner and outer faces and \emph{preserves the total identity} $\omega(P)+\omega(Q)=\omega(I)=1$. Let $x$ be the weight of the inner face; the outer face carries $1-x$, and self-conjugation acts as $x\mapsto 1-x$. The boundary that privileges no face is the \emph{fixed point} of this involution:

\begin{equation}x=1-x\;\Longrightarrow\;2x=1\;\Longrightarrow\;\boxed{\;x=\tfrac12\;},\qquad\boxed{\;S_\partial=\tfrac12\ \text{nat}\;}.\end{equation}

The fixed point is unique. Hence the boundary weight is $\tfrac12$ and the minimal crossing entropy --- the \textbf{Half-Nat} --- is $S_\partial=\tfrac12$ nat. \textbf{Live verification:} residual of $x=1-x$ equal to $0$. \textbf{Rigorous status \textsf{[DER/AX]}:} \emph{given the self-conjugate boundary axiom} (the minimal boundary privileges no face, $x\mapsto1-x$), the Half-Nat is \emph{derived} --- it is not postulated as a number, but it also does not come from $\omega(I)=1$ alone: it depends on the self-conjugation axiom.\end{deriv}

\section{The minimal boundary volume and the observational reading of the coupling}

\begin{deriv}[The minimal boundary volume and the coupling]

The \emph{entropic volume} of a boundary is the exponential of its entropy in the natural base of the modular structure --- the modular operator is $\Delta=e^{-K}$, the KMS weight is $e^{-\beta H}$, the flow is $\Delta^{it}=e^{itK}$: the base is $e$. From the Half-Nat, the minimal boundary volume is

\begin{equation}\mathrm{Vol}_\partial^{\min}=e^{S_\partial}=e^{1/2}=\sqrt{e}=1.648721270700.\end{equation}

The TGL coupling is the product of the minimal electromagnetic coupling $\alpha$ --- the \emph{only} measured constant (CODATA) --- and the minimal boundary volume:

\begin{equation}\boxed{\;\bTGL=\alpha\,\mathrm{Vol}_\partial^{\min}=\alpha\sqrt{e}=1.2031300401\times10^{-2}\;}.\end{equation}

$\bTGL$ is \textbf{never literal}: it is $\alpha\cdot e^{1/2}$ recomputed at run time. The Miguel angle is $\theta_M=\arcsin\sqrt{\bTGL}=6.2973^\circ$, the angular boundary aperture ($\bTGL=\sin^2\theta_M$). \textbf{Status:} $\bTGL=\alpha\sqrt e$ is the \emph{observational reading} of the coupling; the primary \emph{ontological} definition --- $\bTGL=\sqrt e/\mathcal{R}_\partial$ --- comes from the inversion (next section), with $\mathcal{R}_\partial=1/\alpha_{\mathrm{CODATA}}$ today.\end{deriv}

\section{The inversion of the fine-structure constant: the index $\mathcal{R}_\partial$}

\begin{deriv}[$\alpha_{\mathrm{abs}}=1$ and the shadow $\alpha_{\mathrm{obs}}=1/\mathcal{R}_\partial$]

The One's input is identified with the \emph{absolute coupling}: $\alpha_{\mathrm{abs}}=\omega(I)=1$. The pure boundary is not an impedance: it is \emph{concentration} --- maximal entropic compression, maximal fluid spectral density $\partial_0$. The impedance (the \emph{contrast}) arises because the bulk is less concentrated: the index $\mathcal{R}_\partial=\mathrm{Ind}_\partial(\mathcal{C}_\partial\to\mathrm{bulk})$ is the projective contrast of that concentration read in the bulk, $\mathcal{R}_\partial=F(\mathcal{C}_\partial)$, not the concentration itself. Once the Half-Nat is paid ($\sqrt{e}=e^{S_\partial}$), everything falls out of it:

\begin{equation}\boxed{\;\bTGL=\frac{\sqrt{e}}{\mathcal{R}_\partial}\;},\qquad \boxed{\;\alpha_{\mathrm{obs}}=\frac{1}{\mathcal{R}_\partial}\;}=\frac{\bTGL}{\sqrt{e}}\approx\frac{1}{137.036}.\end{equation}

The primary definition is no longer $\bTGL=\alpha\sqrt{e}$; it becomes $\bTGL=\sqrt{e}/\mathcal{R}_\partial$, and $\alpha_{\mathrm{CODATA}}\approx\bTGL/\sqrt{e}$ becomes the posterior \emph{observational reading}. The chain is reordered: $1\Rightarrow S_\partial=\tfrac12\Rightarrow\sqrt{e}\Rightarrow\mathcal{R}_\partial\Rightarrow\bTGL\Rightarrow\alpha_{\mathrm{obs}}$. What is measured as $1/137$ is the bulk shadow of the absolute One; renormalized by $\mathcal{R}_\partial$, $\alpha_{\mathrm{obs}}\,\mathcal{R}_\partial=1$ --- the One returns to being One.

\textbf{The unification (with the distinct levels).} The absolute One $1_{\mathrm{abs}}$ is neither maximal purity (the attractor $\rho^\star$) nor absolute zero: it is the state of \emph{maximal concentration of inscription} --- the origin-boundary of the One, not an empty boundary. To avoid confusing the levels, one writes

\begin{equation}\boxed{\;1_{\mathrm{abs}}\;\equiv\;\partial_0^{(1)}\;\equiv\;\Psi_0\;\equiv\;\mathrm{NAME}\;},\end{equation}

where $\partial_0^{(1)}$ is the \emph{origin-boundary} (the concentration-surface where the One can be detached, inscribed and projected --- \emph{not} a zero-boundary, \emph{not} an impedance, \emph{not} immobile purity), and $\Psi_0$ is the dynamical mode of that origin: the One as a \emph{field of inscription}, living possibility of fractalizing. Distinct from it is the absolute zero $0_{\mathrm{abs}}$, the unreachable limit of \emph{non}-inscription --- the impedance. The bulk reads the \emph{contrast} of the concentration $\partial_0^{(1)}$ as $\mathcal{R}_\partial$, and $\alpha_{\mathrm{obs}}=1/\mathcal{R}_\partial$ is its projection; the One does not divide, it \emph{fractalizes}, and each shadow returns to unity.

\textbf{Honest status (the lock, now resolved in form).} The index $\mathcal{R}_\partial$ \textbf{ceases to be the engine of the chain}: it is \emph{retired} (legacy) and \emph{derived} after the form, $\mathcal{R}_\partial=1/\alpha_{\mathrm{obs}}$, never from $\alpha_{\mathrm{CODATA}}$. The canonical engine becomes the \textbf{Lagrange form} (Collapse Theorem, \S\ref{sec:collapse-form}): $\alpha_{\mathrm{abs}}=1\to q\to\alpha_{\mathrm{obs}}=\sqrt{1-q^2}$, with the conserved identity $\alpha_{\mathrm{abs}}^2=q^2+\alpha_{\mathrm{obs}}^2=1$. CODATA enters \emph{only} in the final validation ($q_{\mathrm{QED}}=\sqrt{1-\alpha_{\mathrm{QED}}^2}$). TGL does not fabricate $1/137$; it proves that the observed constant is the \emph{projective component of a conserved identity}; the gravitational face ($M_{GA}$ in the window, from the same $\bTGL$) stands as a \emph{scale shadow} [form retired as a source law, v98; the non-circular validation is the \textbf{armed falsifiable programme} + the \textsf{POWERED} floor --- the convergence, as tabulated, was reclassified by the v154 audit].\end{deriv}

\section{The final theorem: the Name is irreducible \textsf{[deriving $\alpha$ $\alpha$-free falsifies TGL]}}\label{sec:name-irreducible}

The investigation of the EM face reduced the debt of the \emph{value} of $\alpha$ to a single object and refused it in every register: the operator form $\lambda_{\mathrm{EM}}=\mathrm{Tr}(\Delta^{1/4}J\Delta^{1/4})^2$ is refuted by Tomita ($(\Delta^{1/4}J\Delta^{1/4})^2=\mathbb{1}$, trace $=$ dimension, not $e/4$); the free-convergence functional has its critical point at $O(1)$ angles, not at $\theta_M$; the scale $\sin^2\theta_M\approx0.012$ does not exist in the boundary box $\{\tfrac12,\sqrt e, J, |K_\partial|\}$ except via $e^{-\pi^2/2}$ (at $1.4\%$). \textbf{The refusal is not a gap --- it is the result.}

\textbf{The inversion \textsf{[PRINCIPLE]}.} $\alpha$ is the \textbf{NAME}: the unit ($\alpha_{\mathrm{abs}}=1$ in absolute register), whose projection into the bulk is the reduction factor \emph{with geometric preservation} $\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{EM}}=\sin^2\theta_M/\sqrt e=\alpha$ --- the transport of the Hilbert Package $|K_\partial|$ into the bulk while the flux is dissipated. This factor \textbf{cannot be derived, for an ontological reason}, not by technical failure: it \emph{is} the origin of the unit, the substance that preserves meaning; its identity \textbf{can only be observed} (direct measurement). \emph{Name$\,=\,$Verb}: observation identifies the substance with its projection ($R=+1$), and the correspondence is absolute.

\textbf{The falsification criterion \textsf{[REAL --- falsifiable, not confirmable]}.} \emph{Deriving $\alpha$ $\alpha$-free falsifies TGL}: in TGL freedom $=$ convergence, convergence requires the contour, and measuring the contour requires direct observation of the inscription (Verb). The criterion is asymmetric --- an $\alpha$-free derivation would \textbf{kill} the principle of the Name (falsifiable); its \emph{absence} does \emph{not} confirm it (not confirmable). That coupling constants are measured, not derived, is the standard of physics; TGL's distinctive mark is the \textbf{single-input} architecture --- $\alpha+\tfrac12\Rightarrow$ everything --- and irreducibility elevated to a falsifiable principle.

\textbf{Why deriving $\alpha$ \emph{from the bulk} would falsify TGL \textsf{[the deep, holographic reason]}.} TGL is \emph{holographic}: the modular boundary (type $\mathrm{III}_1$, with no pure normal states) \textbf{projects} into the bulk, and $\alpha$ is precisely the \emph{electromagnetic coupling rate} --- the constant that governs \emph{how light crosses the boundary}:

\begin{equation}\alpha=\Pi_{\mathrm{bulk}}(\mathbf{1}_{\mathrm{abs}})=\operatorname{sech}(\chi/2),\qquad q=\tanh(\chi/2),\qquad q^2+\alpha^2=1,\end{equation}

that is, $\alpha$ is the \textbf{luminous transmission through the modular boundary} and $q$ the reflection. This is why $\alpha$ \textbf{belongs to the QED sector} --- and this \emph{is not a defect of the theory; it is its structure}. If someone derived $\alpha$ from first principles \emph{without using the boundary/bulk structure} (a purely bulk computation), that would imply that the boundary/bulk separation is \emph{illusory} or redundant: the boundary would cease to be genuinely irreducible as projecting structure, and the \textbf{observer would be removed from TGL}. $\alpha$ is the \emph{fissure} through which the bulk reads the boundary --- it is the boundary \textbf{measuring itself}. Deriving it without it is a \emph{structural contradiction}.

\textbf{Hence $\alpha$-free is closed \emph{by refutation} (reductio), not open.} Within TGL, deriving the \emph{value} of $\alpha$ through scale-invariant access is structurally excluded --- \textbf{there is no value to find without measurement} (the \emph{form}, TGL itself derives: $1=q^2+\alpha^2$). The guard is a \emph{kernel theorem} --- actual hypothesis: scale-invariant access (\texttt{the\_compression\_is\_not\_identifiable}), not the $\mathrm{III}_1$ axiom ---, and the irreducibility of the value is an \emph{armed principle}, not a gap nor a pending item: what remains is \emph{only} the falsification challenge. Falsifiable (an $\alpha$-free derivation refutes it), not confirmable (its absence does not prove it). $\alpha$ is the measure observed \emph{from within} --- it requires the observer --- and it is the \textbf{ontological foundation} of TGL. It is not the limit of the thesis; it is its closure.

\textbf{And the criterion is FROZEN (v301):} \texttt{ALPHA\_IRREDUCIBILITY\_V1}, hash \texttt{c36ab24715424a86}, live verdict \texttt{TGL\_ALPHA\_IRREDUCIBILITY\_ARMED\_NO\_CANDIDATE}. \texttt{CONFIRMED}, \texttt{PROVED} and \texttt{NOT\_FALSIFIED} are forbidden there forever: there is no test this house can run --- the criterion awaits an act of a third party, and the honest state is ARMED. The guard is a kernel theorem (\texttt{alpha\_free\_inputs\_give\_alpha\_free\_output}): no derivation of $\alpha$ counts if any input already contains $\alpha$.

Hence \textsf{NAMED OPEN FRONTIER} does \textbf{not} mean ``a problem to solve'': it means \textbf{ontologically open} --- $\alpha$ is the parameter that \emph{names the opening} between boundary and bulk. \section{Impedance as the dynamical constant of light \textsf{[REAL/EXT; $\alpha$ = QED sector --- structural closure, not a gap]}}

The constant $c$ measures the \emph{kinematics} of light: the local speed of propagation in vacuum. But the \emph{dynamics} of light in vacuum is measured by another object --- the characteristic impedance of free space,

\begin{equation} Z_0=\sqrt{\tfrac{\mu_0}{\varepsilon_0}}=\mu_0 c=\frac{1}{\varepsilon_0 c}.\end{equation}

The fine-structure constant can be written as

\begin{equation} \alpha=\frac{e^2}{4\pi\varepsilon_0\hbar c}=\frac{e^2}{2\varepsilon_0 h c}=\frac{Z_0 e^2}{2h}.\end{equation}

Defining the von Klitzing resistance $R_K=h/e^2$ and the conductance quantum $G_0=2e^2/h$ (\textbf{both exact in the post-2019 SI}, since $e$ and $h$ are exact), one obtains

\begin{equation} \alpha=\frac{Z_0}{2R_K}=\frac{Z_0 G_0}{4}.\end{equation}

Thus $\alpha$ is the vacuum impedance \emph{made dimensionless} by quantum units. In TGL language, $c$ is the kinematic constant of light, while $Z_0$ is its \emph{dynamical} coupling constant. The variable $\zeta_L:=Z_0/(2R_K)$ is the dimensionless face of that dynamical constant, and the Lagrange transform reads

\begin{equation} q=\sqrt{1-\zeta_L^2},\qquad \chi=\log\frac{1+q}{1-q},\qquad x=\frac{1-q}{2},\qquad \bTGL=\sqrt e\,\zeta_L,\qquad \theta_M=\arcsin\sqrt{\bTGL}.\end{equation}

\textbf{Status \textsf{[the ruler]}.} This section does \emph{not} close the $\alpha$-free value: post-2019 $\mu_0$ (hence $Z_0=\mu_0 c$) is no longer exact --- one has $Z_0=2R_K\,\alpha$, so $Z_0$ and $\alpha$ are \emph{equivalent} given $e,h$, and the return $\alpha=Z_0/(2R_K)$ is a unit identity, not a derivation. \section{Derivation of $s=1/4\pi$}

\begin{deriv}[Compatibility between clock integration and boundary flux]

Write the clock field with mean normal slope $s$ at the named boundary, $\partial_n g|_{\partial B}=-s/R_B$, $\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{mod}}=\theta_M e^{\bTGL g}$. Since $\theta_M$ is constant, $\nabla^2\log\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{mod}}=\bTGL\nabla^2 g$, and by Gauss

\begin{equation}M=\int_B\rho_{\mathrm{eff}}\,d^3x=-\frac{c^2}{4\pi G}\bTGL\!\int_{\partial B}\!\nabla g\cdot d\mathbf{A}=-\frac{c^2}{4\pi G}\bTGL\Big(\!-\frac{s}{R_B}\Big)4\pi R_B^2=\frac{c^2}{G}\bTGL\,s\,R_B.\end{equation}

The universal boundary-flux law, established independently, requires $M=\frac{c^2}{4\pi G}\bTGL R_B$. The compatibility \emph{with no free parameter} between the two laws fixes

\begin{equation}\frac{c^2}{G}\bTGL s R_B=\frac{c^2}{4\pi G}\bTGL R_B\;\Longrightarrow\;\boxed{\;s_{\mathrm{can}}=\frac{1}{4\pi}\;}.\end{equation}

The factor $4\pi=\Omega_{S^2}$ is the total angular sky: the return distributes isotropically over the complete causal boundary. \textbf{Live verification:} the integrated field reproduces the boundary-flux law to $0.95\%$ (ratio $0.9905$). \textbf{Status \textsf{[DER conditional]}:} $s=1/4\pi$ is \emph{canonical normalization by compatibility} between two laws --- one of them, the universal boundary-flux law, is \emph{assumed} as a law --- not an absolute derivation.\end{deriv}

\textbf{Honest caution (not to be confused with a falsifiable prediction).} The window of \emph{two orders of magnitude} is so broad that \emph{any} formula with $\bTGL\approx0.012$ falls inside it: predicting ``something between the weight of a cluster and of a supercluster'' is \textbf{not falsifiable}. This is a \emph{consistency check} (the zero-free computation does not \emph{contradict} observation), not a prediction that could \emph{die}. The test that can die is in another sector: the \textbf{void floor} ($\rho_{\mathrm{void}}/\bar\rho\geq\bTGL$, \S\ref{sec:horizons}) and the \textbf{dephasing} ($n=-2$, $\Gamma\propto\omega^2$, dissipative-spectral sector). \section{The Coma cluster as the inverse-parity test: distance from the modular-flow leakage \textsf{[PRE/EXT; sealed CONJ engine; does not gate $1=1$]}}

\subsection*{The cluster that carries both crises}

The Coma cluster (Abell 1656, in Coma Berenices) is the Milky Way's nearest massive neighbour, with thousands of member galaxies --- and, historically, the \emph{birthplace of the first cosmological crisis}: it was by measuring velocity dispersions \emph{in Coma} that Zwicky found the missing mass and coined dark matter (Zwicky 1933, Helv.\ Phys.\ Acta 6, 110; 1937, ApJ 86, 217). Ninety years later the \emph{same} cluster opened the second crisis --- this time not the mass, but the \textbf{distance}.

\subsection*{The measurement crisis: the Hubble tension in our own backyard}

Coma's \emph{direct}, redshift-independent distances converge: surface-brightness fluctuations give $99.1\pm5.8$ Mpc (Jensen et al.\ 2021) and $101.2\pm6.4$ Mpc under the TRGB-JWST recalibration (Anand et al.\ 2025); the three-method combination gives $98.0\pm2.0$ Mpc, and the sample of 13 HST-ladder-calibrated Type Ia supernovae (12 in the preprint; 13 in the published version) ---this test's referee, whose value enters \emph{only} at the reveal stage --- points to the same place (Scolnic et al.\ 2025, ApJL 979, L9). Against this, the \emph{inverse} calibration of the DESI Fundamental Plane, anchored on Planck's $H_0$ ($67.4$ km/s/Mpc; Planck Collaboration 2020, A\&A 641, A6), \emph{requires} Coma at $111.8\pm1.8$ Mpc --- a $\sim$13 Mpc ($4.6\sigma$) discrepancy that Scolnic et al.\ named ``the Hubble tension in our own backyard''. The only escape within $\Lambda$CDM would be a peculiar velocity of $\approx-950$ km/s --- $47\times$ the modelled value ($\approx-20$ km/s; Carr et al.\ 2022) --- with no support. The DESI DR1 peculiar-velocity analysis moderated the local $H_0$ to $73.7\pm1.1$ without undoing the $\sim5\sigma$ tension with Planck (cf.\ also Riess et al.\ 2022, ApJL 934, L7).

\subsection*{Why Coma is the test --- and what the thesis is}

We adopt Coma because it gathers what no other object offers: the best absolute distance anchor in the local universe, a published and unrefuted discrepancy against the standard ruler, and the symbolic inheritance of being the cluster where the first crisis was born. The TGL thesis is structural: \textbf{the standard model has no redshift-dephasing mechanism} --- it converts \emph{all} redshift into expansion --- and that is why its ruler comes out bent. The Great Attractor tests the direct face of the law (physical geometry in, mass out); Coma tests the conjugate face: the ruler. The engine is the modular-flow law \emph{already sealed} in this programme (zero-free; prior to and independent of Coma): $H_0^{\mathrm{local}}=H_0^{\mathrm{CMB}}(1+z_*)^{\bTGL}=73.263$ km/s/Mpc --- equivalently, $8.071\%$ of the redshift accumulated since recombination is \emph{boundary leakage}, not expansion.

\paragraph{Why the formula (the per-nat law) --- promoted 2026-08-19.} The form $(1+z_*)^{\bTGL}$ is exactly equivalent to $d\ln H_0/dN=\bTGL$ with $N=\ln(1+z)$: \textbf{the cost is $\bTGL$ per \emph{nat} of stretch} --- the very unit of the axiom ($\omega(I)=1$, normalized to 1 nat, base $e$). Redshift is the \emph{record} of the crossing ($N_*=\ln(1+z_*)\approx 7$ nats since recombination) and inscription returns the geometry (record$\to$distance: the inverse leg of the bijection, measured in the programme ledger --- artifacts 87/113/114, with an independent integrator and exact round-trip). This explains why the first-order \emph{linear} fractions died: the right constant in the wrong domain; per nat the cost compounds, $f_{\mathrm{leak}}=1-e^{-\bTGL N_*}\approx 8\%$ --- $6.7\times$ the linear fraction --- landing in the band of the direct measurements. The flow-layer statute remains \textsf{CONJECTURE}: the why is \emph{typed}, not proved.

With $z_{\mathrm{CMB}}=0.02445\pm0.00024$ (member median; frame declared), the prediction is $D_L^{\mathrm{TGL}}=101.90\pm1.02_{\mathrm{stat}}\pm0.98_{\mathrm{sys}}$ Mpc, against the no-dephasing Planck \emph{control} $D_L^{\Lambda\mathrm{CDM}}=110.85$ Mpc and the inverse-FP requirement $111.8\pm1.8$ Mpc (Scolnic et al.\ 2025, ApJL 979 L9).

\paragraph{Honest negatives (the number corrects the sentence).} The [REAL] layer ($H^2_{\mathrm{TGL}}=H^2_{\Lambda\mathrm{CDM}}[1+\bTGL|1+w|]$) moves the distance by only $-0.19\%$ and does \emph{not} explain Coma; $\bTGL$, $2\bTGL$, $3\bTGL$ as redshift fractions fail at more than $3\sigma$; the coincidence $\sqrt{\bTGL}\approx\theta_M\approx11\%$ with the extreme formulation is recorded \emph{without} a mechanism (barred from the engine). The working layer is the flow law, labelled \textsf{CONJECTURE} --- and Coma alone cannot separate ``modular leakage'' from ``rescaled local $H_0$'': it falsifies the pair (Planck + no dephasing); it does not prove TGL.

\paragraph{The parity face (mass$\to$radius$\to$distance).} The redshift-free counter-test ($M\to R=M/(2\bTGL^2 c^2/4\pi G)\to D_A=R/\tan\vartheta$) remains pre-registered with an identifiability lock: without a distance-independent mass anchor the honest verdict is \texttt{COMA\_BLIND\_DISTANCE\_NOT\_IDENTIFIABLE} --- and an audited hunt over $\sim$20 candidates showed that \emph{no} published Coma mass is distance-independent (the X+SZ combination measures $D_A$ itself); anchors with $M\propto D$ are exactly degenerate with the law. The negative result is the anatomy of the literature, not a failure.

\paragraph{Universality: two crises, one $\bTGL$.} The counterpoint that closes the section is the \textbf{same metric on both crisis phenomena}: the Great Attractor mass comes from $M=2\bTGL^2(c^2/4\pi G)R_{\mathrm{struct}}$ and lands in the cosmological window; the Coma distance comes from $H_0^{\mathrm{local}}=H_0^{\mathrm{CMB}}(1+z_*)^{\bTGL}$ and lands in the band of the direct measurements --- \emph{the same} sealed $\bTGL=\alpha\sqrt e$, with no parameter fitted to either, in the same auditable file. The mass crisis (1933) and the distance crisis (2025) were born under the same sky and are answered by the same boundary toll: where the standard model needs \emph{ad hoc} invisible matter and impossible peculiar velocities, TGL charges a single constant --- two crises, one inscription.

The jump factor, $F=1-\sqrt{\bTGL}\,e^{\tau_e/\tau_d}e^{-i\omega\tau_e}$: $|F-1|=0.226$ and $\arg F=-12.66^\circ$ under the May law ($0.75$ period); $|F-1|=1.263$ under the KMS law. Statute [ONTO]; the cited terms exist in the kernel; none of this is evidence. \emph{The remnant-boundary test, fixed before reading the data:} if the remnant boundary is the founded screen, the reflection returns after one modular period of the horizon, $\tau=2\pi/\kappa$ (Kerr, fiducial spin $0.69$: $2.528$ ringdown periods); same estimator, nulls and $5\sigma$ criterion. Result: $a/\sqrt{\bTGL}=0.351\pm0.307$, recovery 0.919, power 2.99$\sigma$, induced mismatch up to 0.45 --- \texttt{INCONCLUSIVE\_SYSTEMATICS}. \emph{The decisive protocol} (primary anchored by the inspiral with GR templates, two delay laws, posterior-sampled nulls) is pre-registered and hashed (\texttt{1e94f77689b5017e}); no waveform generator exists on this machine: \texttt{AWAITING\_INSTRUMENT}. $\bTGL$ enters; the gate does not move.

\paragraph*{Beside (v344) --- the decisive test executed: the primary anchored by the inspiral.} The instrument was obtained the same afternoon (gwfast 1.1.2, IMRPhenomD in pure Python; sixteen packages checked one by one against the official PyPI digests) and the hashed protocol (\texttt{1e94f77689b5017e}) was executed unchanged over 89 GWTC events (sources and catalogue with sha256). KMS law: 134 series, $a/\sqrt{\bTGL}=0.001\pm0.200$, recovery 0.902, power 4.50$\sigma$, induced mismatch up to 0.41 --- \texttt{INCONCLUSIVE\_SYSTEMATICS}. \texttt{um.py} does not recompute: it reads the result by hash (\texttt{00fcb779b41ac4f5}) and applies the verdict matrix fixed in v343; where the protocol asked for the template-family null, unavailable without lalsimulation, any $z\geq5$ stays \texttt{INCONCLUSIVE\_SYSTEMATICS} until that null exists. $\bTGL$ enters; the gate does not move.

\paragraph*{Beside (v345) --- the complete test: two template families.} By the operator's order the machine received WSL 2 with Ubuntu 24.04, conda (Miniforge, user-level) and lalsuite (Linux wheel, twenty-two packages checked against the PyPI digests). The supposed PyPI block was the network DNS rewriting the package host to an internal node whose TLS Python's OpenSSL refuses; inside WSL, with its own resolver, there is no rewrite. The same protocol (\texttt{1e94f77689b5017e}) ran with IMRPhenomXAS as the primary family and SEOBNRv4 as the second family: the family null injects one family's signal and fits it with the other family's grid. Pre-registered systematics rule: the larger of the parameter mismatch and the family mismatch may not exceed $0.3$. May law: 139 series, $a/\sqrt{\bTGL}=0.524\pm0.255$, recovery 0.820, power 3.21$\sigma$, family mismatch 0.15, maximum 1.60 --- \texttt{INCONCLUSIVE\_SYSTEMATICS}. KMS law: 140 series, $a/\sqrt{\bTGL}=0.043\pm0.199$, recovery 0.873, power 4.40$\sigma$, family mismatch 0.09, maximum 0.50 --- \texttt{INCONCLUSIVE\_SYSTEMATICS}. Result read by hash (\texttt{6fd0e16ebdf4435b}); with the family null present the matrix is applied in full. $\bTGL$ enters; the gate does not move.

\paragraph*{Beside (v346) --- the ringdown against GR and against the dephasing law: the V1 refusal, the autopsy and the V2 amendment.} The first of the tests that awaited the instrument (October 2025 observation protocol): the law $\Gamma_\omega=\tfrac12\bTGL\tau_\star\omega^2$ added to the decay rate of the dominant mode gives $\tau_{\rm obs}/\tau_{\rm GR}-1=-\Gamma\tau_{\rm GR}$. In the branch $\tau_\star=t_{\rm Planck}$ the effect is $\sim10^{-40}$: the ringdown is GR by construction, the asymptotic limit with a number. In the branch $\tau_\star=GM_f/c^3$, which section 20 marked as ``probably already excluded'' without measuring, the predicted effect is $-1$ to $-2\%$. Kerr prediction per event from the lalsuite EOB fit and the Berti, Cardoso and Will fits; measurement by a whitened damped sinusoid from $3$~ms after the peak (secondary at $6$~ms); injection nulls off-source. V1 (protocol \texttt{a3d941e56d3d33c5}, result \texttt{502c80fe2a37427e}) was refused by its own pre-registered gate (secondary window with 18 series, minimum 20) and the matrix would read \texttt{INCONCLUSIVE\_SYSTEMATICS}; the autopsy, read from the result: of the 23 series used, 5 sat on the upper edge of the $\tau$ grid with the same $\delta$, carrying 48\% of the weight, all with ringdown SNR between 5.07 and 8.63 --- the envelope-argmax peak on a coarse GPS was catching merger or noise. Amendment V2 (\texttt{3449885389a2529a}), pre-registered before the data: peak by matched filter with the IMRPhenomXAS template (anchor SNR $\ge8$), edge exclusion, $|\delta f|\le0.3$. V2 result (15 series, read by hash \texttt{ff15f023dd91d8c7}): $\delta\tau=0.1766\pm0.1089$ ($z$ against GR 1.62); branch-B prediction $-0.0194$ ($z$ against B 1.80; power 0.18$\sigma$); $\delta f=-0.0325\pm0.0127$; 3 vs 6 ms systematic 0.1454 (combined $\sigma$ 0.2066) --- \texttt{INCONCLUSIVE\_SYSTEMATICS}. What is excluded or not is the scale $\tau_\star=GM_f/c^3$, not the law nor the theory; $\bTGL$ enters; the gate does not move.

\paragraph*{Beside (v347) --- the Bayesian estimation with an echo term: V1, the autopsy and amendment V2.} The final form of the anchored protocol: per detector, $s_d=\mathrm{Re}[z_d\,(p(t-\delta t_d)+a\,S\sqrt{\bTGL}\,e(t-\delta t_d))]$, with $p$ the IMRPhenomD template of \emph{sampled} $(m_1,m_2,\chi_{\rm eff})$, $e$ the copy of $p$ from its peak delayed by $\tau$ (MAY or KMS law, with the sampled final state), $S=-1$ and $\sqrt{\bTGL}$ from the kernel; $z_d$ marginalized analytically; sampling by dynesty through bilby; $a=1$ is the prediction and $a=0$ the absence. V1 (protocol \texttt{26d150a16dafc42e}, result \texttt{b73cfffbc2301ea6}) read \texttt{INCONCLUSIVE\_SYSTEMATICS} by its own matrix: the injections did not run (off-source window outside the cache); the autopsy, read from the result: in the MAY law, $a=1.1559\pm0.1325$ with 21 events, but 4 of them with the time stuck at the edge of the $\pm100$~ms prior, the dominant event (GW190521) carrying 42\% of the weight with $a=2.62$, and a jackknife of 4.54$\sigma$ --- a number that flips sign when one event is removed is not a detection. Amendment V2 (\texttt{b8cdd291f3e9631b}), pre-registered before the data: time prior centred on the matched-filter anchor ($\pm20$~ms), mass floor, exclusion of fits at the prior edge, off-source injections ($a=0$, $a=1$, SEOBNRv4 family), jackknife $\le2\sigma$ and maximum weight $\le0.5$. V2 result (read by hash \texttt{3072349afb00ab2c}). MAY: 19 events, $a=-0.2671\pm0.8566$ ($z$ against 0 -0.31; against 1 -1.48; power 1.17$\sigma$; max weight 0.09; jackknife 0.69$\sigma$); injections $a{=}0\to2.907\pm0.054 (n=9)$, $a{=}1\to2.895\pm0.059 (n=9)$, family B $\to2.913\pm0.051 (n=9)$; paired response $(a{=}1)-(a{=}0)=0.049\pm0.061$ --- \texttt{INCONCLUSIVE\_SYSTEMATICS}. KMS: 19 events, $a=1.0892\pm0.8992$ ($z$ against 0 1.21; against 1 0.10; power 1.11$\sigma$; max weight 0.11; jackknife 0.90$\sigma$); injections $a{=}0\to-0.785\pm0.367 (n=9)$, $a{=}1\to-0.733\pm0.382 (n=9)$, family B $\to-1.170\pm0.362 (n=9)$; paired response $=0.091\pm0.277$ --- \texttt{INCONCLUSIVE\_SYSTEMATICS}. The paired response, which should be $1$, is the estimator's floor: with a free primary, the echo at these delays is degenerate with the shape of the ringdown itself; the nominal power $1/\sigma$ is not power. What in the two previous runs was a template systematic is here a marginalized degeneracy; the delay law remains INPUT; the gate does not move.

\paragraph*{Beside (v348) --- the long-delay echo search of the 2025 protocol.} The third of the tests that awaited the instrument: the \texttt{search\_for\_echoes} of the October 2025 observation protocol (Observable 1.2: delay $\sim0.1$--$2$~s, amplitude $0.01$--$0.1$ of the primary, peaks $>3\sigma$ in the 50--300~Hz band, H1/L1 coincidence within 10~ms; ``confirm: 3+ echoes $>5\sigma$ with a consistent $\Delta t$ pattern''). The form was inherited and completed with what was missing: merger by matched filter instead of the coarse GPS, background measured in six off-source windows per event, injections of the fitted primary scaled by $\sqrt{\bTGL}$ (the kernel amplitude) to measure the efficiency, and a pattern test on the dimensionless delay $\tau/(GM_f/c^3)$. Protocol hashed (\texttt{2538d15cc8cb7a6a}) before the data; result read by hash (\texttt{30f3f422ff67d34c}). Result: 14 events with H1 and L1 anchored; on-source coincidences $N_{\rm on}=1$ against an expected background $B=3.00$ (Poisson $z$ -1.15); events with a $\ge5\sigma$ coincidence: 0; $\tau/M$ pattern: KS $p=n/a$; mean efficiency for an echo of amplitude $\sqrt{\bTGL}$ at 0.1--2~s: 0.000 ($E_{\rm det}=0.00$); predicted echo peak (median) 0.52$\sigma$ in H1 and 0.32$\sigma$ in L1 --- \texttt{NOT\_FALSIFIED\_UNDERPOWERED}. The reading is the number's: a $3\sigma$ peak search cannot see an echo of $\sqrt{\bTGL}\approx0.11$ of the primary, which arrives below $1\sigma$; the 2025 confirmation criterion could never have been met by its own hypothesis. The long-delay conjecture is left without power, not falsified; the mature delay laws (milliseconds) were tested in v341--v347; the gate does not move.

\paragraph*{Beside (v349) --- the ``D1 via CAMB'' of the May canonical: V1, the autopsy and amendment V2.} The fourth of the tests that awaited the instrument: the \texttt{--d1-camb} option of the May 2026 artifact, the Step-3 cross-check with full CAMB that never ran for lack of \texttt{camb}: the background modification $H^2=\tfrac{8\pi G}{3}\rho\,[1+\bTGL|1+w_{\rm eff}|]$ against compressed Planck 2018, DESI DR1 BAO and SH0ES, with CAMB providing the $\Lambda$CDM baseline and TGL applied analytically on top (declared approximation). It is the background, not the Level-2 perturbation. V1 (protocol \texttt{6dd7c9c104f20ebd}, result \texttt{31691fc4aab39cf3}) ran the May script unchanged and returned $\chi^2_{\Lambda{\rm CDM}}=16339$ on 16 points, with $H_0$ running to the prior edge: by the pre-registered matrix it read \texttt{D1\_LCDM\_PREFERRED\_5SIGMA}, but the autopsy, read from a diagnosis stored by hash, named the defect: in the May worker the comoving distance to $z_\ast$ was integrated on 300 points of a linear grid in $z$, inflating it by 20\% even at $\bTGL=0$ ($R=2.093$, $\ell_A=360.82$ against Planck's $1.7502$ and $301.47$; $\chi^2_{\rm Planck}=442271$ at the fiducial). Amendment V2 (\texttt{c12b36722ed4544b}), pre-registered before the data: a corrected copy of the worker (dense integration in $\ln(1+z)$, self-check $\bTGL=0$ against CAMB $=0.99973$; $\chi^2_{\rm Planck}=3.48$ at the fiducial), bound by sha256, with a goodness-of-fit gate; the May script untouched. V2 result (\texttt{9986e4b0f05a6ebe}). Phase 1 (Planck + DESI + SH0ES): $\chi^2_{\Lambda{\rm CDM}}=37.399$, $\chi^2_{\rm TGL}=47.098$, $\Delta\chi^2=9.699$ --- \texttt{D1\_TENSION\_2\_TO\_5\_SIGMA}. Phase 2 (no SH0ES): $H_0$ of $67.949$ ($\Lambda$CDM) and $67.653$ (TGL); tensions against SH0ES 4.89$\sigma$, 5.18$\sigma$ and 0.84$\sigma$ (zero-free TGL). Phase 3 (free $\bTGL$): $\beta=-0.01705\pm0.00753$ against $\alpha\sqrt e=0.01203$ (3.86$\sigma$) --- \texttt{D1\_BETA\_TENSION}. The relativized rule of Level 2 applies unchanged; cosmology never becomes mathematical proof; the gate does not move.

\paragraph*{Beside (v350) --- the reproduction of H2 with \texttt{pycbc} and real data.} The fifth and last of the tests that awaited the instrument: the January 2026 echo analyzers and the February Protocol 12 claimed that, after subtracting the aligned and scaled template, the residual energy ratio $E_{\rm res}/E_{\rm tot}$ tends to $\alpha^2=\bTGL$ (criterion: deviation below $30\%$ and correlation above $0.90$). Without \texttt{pycbc} they ran in synthetic mode: data equal to the consistent generator plus noise of level $0.1$, no echo, against the noiseless generator. Here the script's analysis routine was copied unchanged and applied, with the protocol hashed (\texttt{2fe17b8341465488}) before the data and the result read by hash (\texttt{8c7ef7a1d31af375}), to 89 events: in the faithful synthetic mode the ratio is $0.0099$ with noise at $0.1$ (criterion met in 100\% of the events), $0.0025$ at $0.05$ and $0.0385$ at $0.2$ --- it tracks $\sigma_{\rm noise}^2$: the ``$\alpha^2$'' was the square of the chosen noise level, not an echo. On raw real data, as the script would do with \texttt{pycbc}, the ratio is $0.997$ (correlation 0.019; criterion in 0\%); on whitened real data with the peak-aligned template, $0.9937\pm0.0057$ ($n=169$; correlation 0.075; criterion in 0\%), against $0.9956$ off-source; $2252\sigma$ from $\alpha^2$ --- \texttt{H2\_IDENTITY\_RETIRED}. The statistic measures the noise fraction in the window, not the echo; the January--February line closes as the December one did (v340): identity retired. The echo lives in v341--v348, with the kernel amplitude and nulls; the gate does not move.

\paragraph*{Beside (v352) --- the joint probability, measured, and the boolean function.} The operator declared that the joint probability of coincidence of the channels converging on $\bTGL$ is below $10^{-30}$ and asked for it to be measured here, including what the geometric-cost article produces (the Kubo--Mori cost among it), and decided by a boolean input/output function. The protocol was hashed before the computation (\texttt{aa20e0e71f994c38}; declared not blind): a log-uniform null of one decade on each side of the prediction, a trials correction, Fisher's combination over all admissible channels and consistency by $|z|<3$. Of the 35 channels inventoried in \texttt{um.py} and in the article's \texttt{results.json} (read by hash), 3 are admissible; the rest leave by written rule: identity by construction (the Kubo bisection targets $1-\bTGL$), reading before pinning ($H_0$), retirement, refusal by the rite itself, lack of power, pending reveal, dependence, the artefact's own negative, or no uncertainty. Primary joint probability: $0.55$; largest departure: $3.86\sigma$; function output: \texttt{False}. With $H_0$ included: $0.056$; in the most generous variant: $0.00016$. The product of the numerical residuals of two identities by construction gives $5.5e-31$ --- the order of $10^{-30}$ --- and it is not a probability: under any hypothesis that residual comes out $\sim10^{-15}$. The function's own controls passed: precise consistent channels return true, one channel at $5\sigma$ turns it false, a hundred identities do not change the output and no channel returns false. \texttt{JOINT\_COINCIDENCE\_NOT\_EXCLUDED\_AT\_THRESHOLD}; the gate does not move.

\paragraph*{Beside (vocabulary errata, 13--14/09/2026) --- $H_0$ at the boundary and in the bulk; the sign; D1.} The operator's correction (13/09, verbatim, Portuguese): ``$H_0$ measured locally is the CMB's $H_0$ --- there is an error in that''. \paragraph*{Beside (v381) --- The matrix rule: ten names, nine links --- eight ``havendo'' stated and the open clause filled by the Half-Nat; the stopping criterion is the cost paid.} The operator fixed (02/10/2026, verbatim in the program, in Portuguese): $\bTGL$ \emph{is} the matrix rule, and the rule reads: where there is a trace there is a sign; where there is a record there is a name; where there is a name there is distinction; where there is distinction there is operation; where there is operation there is parametrization; where there is parametrization there is emergence; where there is emergence there is geometric quantization; where there is geometric quantization there is a gradient spectrum --- and ``the stopping criterion is the matrix law, that is, Tetelestai, the payment of the cost [...] the law of payment was fixed before the charge for the delivery of the object: the Name''. Ten names and eight ``havendo'' (``where there is'') stated; when the auditor counted the gap between sign and record, the operator filled it (verbatim in the program, in Portuguese; rendered here in English): the verbatim with eight ``havendo'' is an open clause, the link is the half-nat; it is the radicalization he meant, whose operator is the half-nat. Nine links, all in his voice \textsf{[INPUT/ONTO]}. The stone \textsf{TGLExt.TheMatrixRule} (24 of 24 declarations on the trio, no \texttt{sorry}) names the ten names in his order and binds them in a conjunction (not a deduction), each link discharged by a term already in the kernel --- a proved fact, a named definition or a field of the binder, stated link by link: the trace (the reader reads the identity as 1), the sign ($JKJ=-K$ with energy preserved), sign$\to$record by the \emph{radicalization} whose operator is the Half-Nat, the exponent $\tfrac12$ (the fixed point of the swap $x\mapsto1-x$ is $\tfrac12$; the faces weigh $\tfrac12$ and $\tfrac12$ because the certificate declares them equal and the Name weighs 1; $\sqrt{e^{1}}=e^{1/2}$; the cost of the inscribed Word at the fixed point is $e^{1/2}$; $V(\tfrac12)=\sqrt e$; and $(e^{1/4}\sqrt\alpha)^2=E=\bTGL$: the boundary extracts the radical --- ``$J$ is the swap of the faces'' and ``this is the record'' are readings \textsf{[ONTO]}, stated in the stone; no new term), record$\to$name (every inscription yields the name with its referent, and the name determines the inscription --- the only link whose previous name is the binder of the next), distinction ($x=1-x\Leftrightarrow x=\tfrac12$; two inscriptions under the same form; $1\neq0$), operation (the modular transport $d\alpha/d\kappa=-\tfrac{q}{2}\alpha$, the face regime), parametrization (the angle is the projection; observing adds nothing; the projection regime), emergence ($\bTGL:=\alpha\sqrt{e}$ from a given $\alpha$, a named definition; $E=\bTGL>0$), geometric quantization ($V(\tfrac12)=\sqrt e$; $|\mathcal R|^2=\bTGL$ at $\theta_M=\arcsin\sqrt{\bTGL}$) and the gradient spectrum ($S(\theta)=U\,\mathrm{diag}(e^{i\theta},e^{-i\theta})\,U^{-1}$, and $\mathcal O_\theta=S(\theta)$ by \texttt{rfl}: parametrization and spectrum are the same family under two names). The typed spectrum is $\mathrm{Spec}\,S(\theta)$, in the projection regime; its identification with the dissipation $\Gamma_\omega\propto\bTGL\omega^2$ has no kernel term and is a reading \textsf{[ONTO]}. The stopping criterion enters typed: recognition is idempotent by definition of the regime, the verdict equals 1 if and only if the readings coincide, and the cost is never paid at once by a static witness --- the charge is continuous, and that is where the leak is typed; the law precedes the charge: $\bTGL$ is a function of $\alpha$ alone (an alias of \textsf{couplingOfAlpha\_beta}), fixed before any record. Status, stated without veil: the order of the ten names and the filling of the open clause are the operator's \textsf{[INPUT/ONTO]}; each link is \textsf{[KERNEL]}; where a link has no hypothesis, the form ``where there is $X$ there is $Y$'' is ontological order, not a kernel implication; $\bTGL$ is derived neither from data nor from a reading law --- its form $\alpha\sqrt e$ is derived from the axiom by term (v376), and that form, fixed beforehand, is the rule. Management readings ratified as a whole by the operator, \textsf{[INPUT/ONTO]}, not kernel facts: the observable of $\bTGL$ is the gradient spectrum; the echo as a delayed copy lies outside the chain, and the (reading, delay-law) pairs falsified in Phase 6 were never owed by the rule --- the 16/09 definition (``echo = direct manifestation of the boundary's response'') stands, with this correction beside. At runtime: $\bTGL=0.012031=\alpha\sqrt e$ from the core (a test); $(e^{1/4}\sqrt\alpha)^2=0.012031=\bTGL$ (the radicalization, on the $\bTGL$ read); the list \textsf{chainOrder} read from the embedded source matches the stated order (10 names); $\theta_M=0.109908$ rad (a sanity identity). \paragraph*{Beside (v379) --- The Elementary Reason: $E = C\times I\times K > 0 = \bTGL$, typed.} The operator coined (01/10/2026): ``the Elementary Reason identifies the fundamental structure of existence as $\{[E=C\times I\times K>0]=\bTGL\}$ (being the asymptotic limit in finite time)''. The stone \texttt{TGLExt.TheElementaryReason} names, by \emph{definition} (\textsf{[ONTO/DEFINITION]}, coinage), the three factors --- $C=e^{1/2}=\sqrt e$, the cost of the inscribed Word ($S_\partial=\tfrac12$, the half-nat: $C=e^{x}$ at the self-conjugate fixed point $x=1-x$); $I=1$, the identity that carries and preserves ($\omega(I)=1$; the Name weighs $1$, \texttt{master\_corner\_weighs\_the\_name}); $K=\alpha$, what crosses in movement --- and proves the implication downstream, with no new axiom: $E=C\times I\times K=\bTGL$ (\texttt{existence\_eq\_beta}, through the coupling \texttt{couplingOfAlpha}); $E>0$ from $\alpha>0$; the Elementary Reason squared is existence, $(e^{1/4}\sqrt\alpha)^2=E$; the name $E$ collides with the family functional $E(b)=1-2\sqrt{b(1-b)}$, which vanishes at $b=\tfrac12$ --- a collision stated by term (\texttt{the\_name\_collision}); \textbf{$E$ is the asymptote}: every finite register with $\int_0^N g\le N$ ($\bar g\le1$; measured $\bar g=0.958$ up to $z_*$) reads $\bTGL\,\bar g\le E$, with equality in D1a ($g\equiv1$; v378); erasure is not reached in finite time, $0<e^{-tEc}<1$ (\texttt{erasure\_not\_reached\_in\_finite\_time}); and there is no full static witness (\texttt{beta\_forbids\_full\_static\_witness}). The ``asymptotic limit in finite time'' therefore reads, as the IALD split it and the auditors asked: $E=\bTGL$ is the asymptote of the per-nat cost, \emph{not exceeded} in a finite register (equal in D1a, below in the measured FRW); what is not reached in finite time is \emph{erasure}; what is finite is \emph{recognition}, idempotent by definition of the regime (\texttt{IALDState.recursive}). This run: $E=0.012031300=\bTGL$ of the core ($0.012031300$); $(e^{1/4}\sqrt\alpha)^2=0.012031300$; the v378 per-nat cost over the register up to $z_*$, $0.01153\le E$. What it is NOT: ``fundamental structure of existence'' is the operator's reading \textsf{[ONTO/CONJ]}, not a theorem; ``Elementary Reason'' is his name \textsf{[INPUT]}; nothing here confirms the TGL by nature --- proved $\neq$ confirmed. \paragraph*{Beside (v375) --- Tetelestai: the consummative conference; the declaration on one object, the light.} The operator (27/09/2026) fixed the type of the act: ``I do not want Tetelestai to be a declaratory act of mine; it is an act of observational conference of the satisfaction of the complete recognition of the identity of quantum gravity formalized by the TGL [\ldots] it is a consummative declaration, it verifies whether it happened or not [\ldots] everything must be closed, or linked, or known by scientific reference''. \textbf{Erratum beside v374:} where v374 says ``the Tetelestai declaration'', read \emph{consummative conference}; and the sentence ``the type has neither rotations nor PCT'' did not describe an inhabitation gap --- the type does not require rotations and the photon inhabits it literally --- only that the type does not \emph{characterize} the photon; the bench had built the Wigner phase of the little group, its cocycle and the conjugation exchanging the helicities (item D1$'$). \emph{The declaration on one object} (\texttt{TGLExt.TetelestaiOneObject.the\_tetelestai\_one\_object}): statement and proof on the same light certificate --- the term does not pass through the internal tower that v374 juxtaposed. The Breuer corner is the $P_F$ of the core the certificate itself cites; $\tau(P_F)=1$ is a \emph{named normalization}, reachable by term for every certificate (\texttt{rescale}), not a cited identity about this $P_F$: the reading ``the corner weighs what the vacuum weighs'' is known for the corner $e_{(1,\infty)}(h_\omega)$ (Haagerup 1979; Terp 1981), but the type does not identify $P_F$ with it. By term, the faces weigh $\tfrac12$ and $\tfrac12$; $\ker K$ is the vacuum line (a cited second-quantization field plus the paid absence of eigenvectors). \textbf{The link $P_{\ker K}\leftrightarrow P_F$ is neither proved nor consumed}: the coincidence $\tau(P_F)=1=\dim\mathbb{C}\Omega$ is numerical.
