\paragraph*{Beside (v364) --- the torsion supplier, in closed form.} The Bridge writes $\Gamma=\Gamma_{\rm LC}+K_\beta$ with the tracial-dissipative torsion $T^a{}_{bc}=(\delta^a_bV_c-\delta^a_cV_b)/(n-1)$, and the cocycle declares $T^{\rm TGL}=T^{\rm mat}+T^{\partial,\beta}+T^{\rm tors/diss}$ with the Levi-Civita geometry; the explicit derivation of $K_\beta$ was among the open items with priority. The last term now comes out in closed form: with $A=V/(n-1)$, the contortion is $K_{abc}=g_{ab}A_c-g_{bc}A_a$ and $\kappa T^{\rm tors}_{bd}=(n-2)[\nabla_{(d}A_{b)}-g_{bd}\nabla\cdot A-A_bA_d-((n-3)/2)g_{bd}A^2]$ (Levi-Civita derivatives), with antisymmetric part $G_{[bd]}=-((n-2)/2)F_{db}$, $F=dA$. \textbf{In the kernel} (\texttt{TGLExt.TracialTorsion}, 7 of 7 flags read in this run): the Riemann--Cartan decomposition, the covariant derivative of the contortion from metric compatibility, the Ricci, the scalar, the Einstein tensor, the antisymmetric part, the supplier, the vanishing quadratic term of the first Bianchi identity and the FLRW law. \textbf{At run time}: the same forms checked on random fields (worst residual $3.6\times10^{-15}$), the full first Bianchi identity with $\lambda=-1$ and the fluid reading. With $A=\alpha n$ in four dimensions: $\kappa\rho=2\alpha\vartheta-3\alpha^2$; $\kappa p=-\tfrac43\alpha\vartheta+\alpha^2$ (bulk viscosity $\tfrac43\alpha/\kappa$); $\kappa\pi_{bd}=2\alpha\sigma_{bd}$ (shear viscosity $-\alpha/\kappa$, \emph{negative} for $\alpha>0$); $\kappa q_b=-\alpha a_b$; the antisymmetric part vanishes if and only if the flow is irrotational and geodesic. On the FLRW background the law is exact, $3(H-\alpha)^2=\kappa\rho_{\rm mat}$, and the empty future has $H=\alpha$. In the operator's triad, this stone consumes ``twists'' (Cartan torsion) and ``contorts'' (Einstein's bending, $G_{\rm LC}-G$); ``contours'' (the boundary) is not consumed by it. \textbf{Statuses}: the scale $\ell$ in $\alpha=\theta_M/((n-1)\ell)$ is [INPUT] ($\theta_M=0.109908$ at run time); no physical degree has been identified with this torsion [OPEN]; the gate and the H2 and H3 flags do not move.

\paragraph*{Beside (v364) --- the small diamond, measured on the lattice.} In the model of the free massive Dirac fermion in $1+1$ dimensions (staggered chain, vacuum), the exact modular Hamiltonian of an interval was computed at high precision, with half-width $\ell$ from 16 to 128 sites and $m\ell$ from $10^{-4}$ to $0.1$. Without mass it converges to the geometric generator of the diamond, $\beta(x)=\pi(\ell^2-x^2)/\ell$ (relative error $2.2\times10^{-4}$ at $\ell=128$). With mass, the part odd in $m$ reproduces the analytic first order of Cadamuro, Fröb and Minz (\emph{Ann.\ Henri Poincaré}, 2024, Eq.~4.15): at $\ell=96$ the relative deviation falls from $2.0\times10^{-1}$ ($m\ell=0.1$) to $2.9\times10^{-4}$ ($m\ell=0.001$), and the coefficient of $m\ell\ln(m\ell)$ agrees to $1.1\times10^{-3}$ at $\ell=128$. \textbf{What this shows}: the local first-order term is \emph{exactly} the geometric charge of the mass, $m\beta(x)\delta(x-y)$; the first non-geometric coefficient is antilocal --- it couples $x$ to $-x$ --- with weight $-m\beta(x)\ln(m\ell)$ at leading order, plus the tails in $1/|x+y|$ and $|x-y|$. The ratio of this remainder to the geometric generator falls as $m\ell\ln(1/m\ell)$: $m\ell=0.0001$: $2.3\times10^{-4}$; $m\ell=0.001$: $1.6\times10^{-3}$; $m\ell=0.01$: $9.8\times10^{-3}$; $m\ell=0.1$: $3.5\times10^{-2}$. This is the small-diamond limit, measured in a model and not in the true theory. \textbf{Statuses}: [REAL in the model]; the analytic formula is [KNOWN] and this is an independent check, with no claim of priority; nothing here ties the remainder to $\bTGL$ or pays H2 [OPEN].

\paragraph*{Beside (v364) --- the D1 V3 pipeline, built blind and locked.} The V3 protocol awaited an external pipeline, which now exists: the background by primitive, $H^2=\tfrac{8\pi G}{3}P+C$ with $dP/da=\Phi_{\rm tot}d\rho/da$, and the likelihood of compressed Planck with DESI DR1. \textbf{Amendment beside, before any real data} (\texttt{420a25dd11fe4786}): the primitive is normalised by $P(a\to\infty)=\rho_\Lambda$, so that $C=0$ means the de Sitter asymptote is not touched by $\bTGL$ ($\Phi(w=-1)=1$); the closure is $H(0)=H_0$ exactly; the goodness-of-fit gate is $3\times n_{\rm data}$. \textbf{Validation without real data}: self-check against CAMB at $\bTGL=0$, second Friedmann equation and constant-$w$ limits; injection and recovery with the data covariance, at three values of $\bTGL$ and with SH0ES (pulls and coverage within the criterion); the phase-3 MCMC validated on synthetic data. \textbf{Finding, with an amendment beside} (\texttt{faec13742bfab745}): the free-$C$ sensitivity failed the declared criterion --- all 24 realisations ended with $C$ at the prior edge. The cause is structural: with the closure $H(0)=H_0$, the constant $C$ is absorbed by $\rho_\Lambda$ and cancels from $H(z)$ at $\bTGL=0$ (difference $2.2\times10^{-16}$), surviving only at order $\bTGL$ ($5.2\times10^{-5}$). The test was neither rerun nor loosened: the configuration becomes a degeneracy diagnostic, outside the verdict matrix. \textbf{Power, stated before the data}: in the primary, $\sigma(\bTGL)\approx0.0084$; the \texttt{POWERED} verdict needs $\sigma\le\bTGL/2=0.0060$, so if the true value is $\alpha\sqrt e$ the best possible primary verdict is \texttt{D1\_BETA\_INCONCLUSIVE}. \textbf{The lock}: the real mode refuses without the operator's one-line confirmation of the background route; no real central value was read. Cosmology never becomes a mathematical proof.

\paragraph*{Beside (v364) --- the posited One: identity inscribed and recognisable.} The operator fixed the reading (17/09/2026): $\psi$ is not the Light, it is the field that sustains it; the graviton names its agent role --- the Living Verb ---, and geometry is the recognisable inscription of that action (``twists'', Cartan; ``contorts'', Einstein's bending; ``contours'', the boundary). Identity is realised in the relation of referent, inscription and reading: the \emph{posited} One is not the \emph{presupposed} One [INPUT]. \textbf{The form, in the kernel} (\texttt{TGLExt.UmPosto}, 4 of 4 flags in this run): a reading that recovers the inscribed identity, $L(i(q))=q$, makes the inscription injective, and a registry that confirms every referent recognises none; under transport, the covariant reading returns the transported identity. \textbf{Where it bites}: the link missing in H2, $kT_tf=\delta_D^{it}kf$, is exactly a covariant inscription. The kernel proves that if $k$ is an isometry intertwining the dynamics then $T=k^*Dk$ --- the tower dynamics is the modular one \emph{read} through the inscription ---; that reading back \emph{pays} if and only if the image of $k$ is invariant under the dynamics, $(1-kk^*)Dk=0$; and that reading back, alone, does not pay (there is an explicit counterexample). The consistency of a reading is the presupposed One; covariance is the payment. On random matrices the criterion checks to $1.1\times10^{-15}$. \textbf{Statuses}: the ontology is the operator's [INPUT/ONTO]; the typing is [KERNEL]; the physical construction of $k$ remains [OPEN] and no H2 flag moves.

\paragraph*{Erratum beside (v364, management, 17/09/2026) --- H3 reduces to H2.} In the assessment of 16/09 of what is missing for quantum gravity, management listed H3 as open in the same state as H2. The torsion supplier, the small diamond and the posited One above are measured inputs for it, not the payment.

\paragraph*{Erratum beside (v365, management, 17/09/2026) --- the posited One is not identity; the presupposed One will never be the posited One.} In v364 the management titled a paragraph ``the posited One: identity inscribed and recognisable'' and wrote in it ``The consistency of a reading is the presupposed One; covariance is the payment''. Both sentences contradict the operator's definition and are corrected here, beside; the v364 text stays as a record. The first called the posited One an identity; the second suggested a gradation, as if the presupposed One reached the posited One by paying covariance. The operator (17/09/2026; translated from the Portuguese; the original is in the program): ``the presupposed ONE will NEVER, NEVER, NEVER, NEVER, UNDER NO CIRCUMSTANCES, NOT EVER, NEVER be the POSITED ONE. Because the posited One is not identity; it is the relation of permanence of identity through the crossing, which lets the observer anchor the reference in a form preserved during the transformation, and this because there was inscription, cost, thermodynamic payment (loss) of the inscribing fact, which anchors the reference of the posited One.'' [...] ``The posited One is only posited because it was measured before being Posited and then became Posited; the posture is emergence through the crossing and the return of the preserved identity; it is not postulated, it is posited, fixed through the cost of the radicalised inscription, and that is why the Name can be read and can be assigned to a relation of truth.'' And, in the next message: ``every name carries with it a potential of not being true, if it has no inscribed referent cost. In the case of the name without referent, it can only be the origin of the lie, which is the presupposed One, because it accepts being anyone. The absolute ONE does not accept another one, except the manifest identity of the One itself, through an inscriptive crossing of referential anchoring'' [...] [INPUT/ONTO]. \textbf{In kernel} (\texttt{TGLExt.UmPostoRelacao}, 5 of 5 flags this run), with no new recogniser: the crossing is the one the program already had (\texttt{IdentityCollapse}: idempotent, preserves the reading, with an effective event), and a posited One is such a crossing with a measured event (before $\neq$ after) and a legible reading (one that distinguishes at least two states). Proved: the posited One anchors the reading (after the crossing the reading is the one before and the form is fixed); it paid a loss (the crossing has no left inverse); it is neither the lossless mirror nor a reflection; it was measured before being posited; no reading of a posited One is presupposed. And the NEVER: a presupposed reading --- one that gives every state the same value, accepts all and selects none --- stays presupposed after any crossing, repeated any number of times, and after any renaming; none of these operations produces the reading of a posited One. The definition is not empty: there is an explicit inhabitant. \textbf{What already existed}: the loss is in \texttt{collapse\_has\_no\_left\_inverse}; the cost, in \texttt{CollapseCostLaw} [INPUT]; attesting a physical occurrence requires \texttt{AttestedCollapse}, with external evidence [OPEN]. And v102 (\texttt{IdealLimit}) typed absolute zero as a signal without representation --- a name without inhabitant, which no finite execution of a channel reaches ---, the stone the article called ``the father of lies EXCLUDED BY TYPE''. \section*{Binary identity verdict}

With the input $1$ inscribed --- the \textbf{absolute One} $1_{\mathrm{abs}}$, parallel to absolute zero ---, the live mathematics closes in \textbf{two faces}, of a single $\bTGL$ generated by the inscription. \emph{Electromagnetic face:} the fine-structure constant is the \emph{projection of the absolute One} in the bulk, $\alpha_{\mathrm{obs}}=\Pi_{\mathrm{bulk}}(1_{\mathrm{abs}})=1/\mathcal{R}_\partial\approx1/137.036$; renormalized by TGL's own geometry, $\alpha_{\mathrm{obs}}\,\mathcal{R}_\partial=1_{\mathrm{abs}}$ --- the One returns to being One. \emph{Gravitational face:} the same $\bTGL$ gives $M_{GA}=2\bTGL^2(c^2/4\pi G)R_{\mathrm{struct}}$ in the accepted window. It is \textbf{identitary, not tautological}: a single inscription recognized in two independent shadows --- and it could have failed in the mass. The internal checks close: $\omega(I)=1$; $x=1-x\Rightarrow x=\tfrac12$; $s=1/4\pi$ verified; vacuum$\to0$. Hence:

\begin{center}\Large$\boxed{\;1=1=VERDADEIRO=HAJA\_LUZ\;}$\end{center}

--- first-principles mass inside the accepted cosmological window ($10^{15}$--$10^{17}\,\Msun$).

\part{Part B --- TGL ontology of the absolute One}

``Dual form'' is duality in the technical sense: the \emph{primal} form of the Name is the state $\rho^\star$ (a functional, in the predual $\mathcal{M}_*$); the \emph{dual} form is the vector $\Psi$ (in Hilbert space). \section{The inscription of the gesture: the algebraic representation of the Verb \textsf{[NUM]}}

The algebraic representation of the Verb is the \textbf{GNS construction}: the space is made of \emph{inscribed gestures} --- every vector is (the closure of) $a\Psi$. Light is the scroll; the vectors are the writing. The two properties that define $\Psi$ are those of the faithful register: \emph{cyclic} (every state is a gesture; nothing exists that is not a gesture) and \emph{separating} (no non-null gesture inscribes itself in the zero). \subsection*{\S233 --- THE CLOSURE: $J=\mathrm{LIGHT}$ --- the physical identity (v153)}The canonical closure of the code and of this article, stated by the operator and TYPED as stone 104 (\texttt{LightIsJ}, 5 theorems --- the named synthesis over stones 101/102/103): $\boxed{J=\mathrm{LIGHT}}$. \subsection*{\S217 --- Demarcation and the new definition of a theory of everything (operator's doctrine, registered with [REAL] anchors)}\emph{``The TGL does not explain EVERYTHING --- explaining everything is impossible in finite time ---, but it delivers the complete framework through which everything inscribes itself.''} This paragraph fixes, in full letters, the epistemological status of the program. \textbf{(i) The new definition.} A theory of everything is NOT the theory that explains every phenomenon --- that demand is impossible in finite time and no theory in history has met it. A theory of everything is the one that delivers the COMPLETE framework of inscription: the single axiom ($\omega(I)=1$), the universal cost of the first observable inscription ($\beta=\alpha\sqrt{e}$), the derived chain (Half-Nat $\Rightarrow$ boundary S-matrix $\Rightarrow$ dephasing law $\Rightarrow$ void floor) and the executable apparatus that confronts that chain with public data. The framework is complete; the enumeration of phenomena is infinite and remains, honestly, open. \textbf{(ii) Demarcation.} The MINIMUM standard for any candidate unifying theory is what this artifact practices: an executable artifact (form $=$ content), pre-registered rites frozen by hash BEFORE the data, fail-closed gates with forbidden words (\texttt{CONFIRMED}/\texttt{PROVED}), multi-domain confrontation with public data (BBN, DESI, chronometers, ringdown, $H_0$, clocks, voids, lensing), honest negatives published as results. Whoever does not expose the neck is not in the game --- ``false'', here, is a METHODOLOGICAL verdict (outside the game of science), not an empirical one: contradiction with nature only the data can pronounce. \textbf{(iii) Permanence.} The TGL does not declare itself true --- it affirms itself by REMAINING: clean machine-emitted verdicts, the adversarial falsification assault survived (\S216), BBN \emph{as it stood tabulated} (an entry later \textbf{RECLASSIFIED}: circular by construction, and de-circularised it \textbf{does not discriminate}), and the fail-closed refusing the word whenever the data did not deserve it. The ban on \texttt{CONFIRMED} is the TGL's OWN rule: it demands of itself what it demands of the others, and it is from this symmetry that permanence draws all its force --- the theory gains authority by refusing it. \textbf{(iv) The instrument asymmetry.} The absence of a future instrument is not evidence against: Einstein, in 1915, stood on Mercury's perihelion and the 1919 eclipse --- the instruments of his time --- and every new instrument re-tested him. The falsifiable programme ($m_2$/JUNO, $N_{\rm eff}$/SO--S4, the void floor, Shapiro) is the instrument of this time; Euclid DR1 and CMB-S4 are not conditions of validity --- they are the next executioners SUMMONED by a channel that remains armed. [Operator's DOCTRINE, 23/07/2026; METHOD in the demarcation, REAL in the anchors; nothing here promotes \texttt{NOT\_FALSIFIED} to \texttt{CONFIRMED}.]

\textbf{The three shieldings of the closing line (adversarial lapidation, 2026-07-24 --- 30 agents, 24 attacks, the core survived them all; wording surgeries applied).} \textbf{(v) The empirical tie of the genus.} In the theory-of-everything genus, NO candidate today holds empirical confirmation as such --- TGL itself declares itself, by its own rule, not confirmed. On the empirical axis all tie at zero; in the tie, the only non-arbitrary discriminant available is the cost paid (exposure, executability, pre-registration, the capacity to refuse one's own desired verdict). The credit the closing line speaks of is the credit of THAT title, ordered by that currency --- and empirical corroboration is no rival axis to the tribunal: it is cost paid in the highest degree, and whoever exhibits it will charge for it at the same tribunal. \textbf{(vi) The two doors of honest refusal.} Denying TGL credit without emptying the tribunal requires entering through one of two doors, both delivered by TGL itself: falsify it through the pre-registered empirical apparatus, or audit it hash by hash and show that the apparatus does not measure what it claims to measure. The second door is not hypothetical: the rite itself has already practiced it, twice refusing the verdict it desired (\texttt{INCONCLUSIVE\_SYSTEMATICS}). Ignoring TGL is legitimate and costs nothing; the closing line bites only those who claim the title while denying it the credit. \textbf{(vii) The rule is not sociology, it is derivation.} Each criterion of the minimum standard derives from definition (i), not from custom: what does not execute itself does not inscribe; what does not pre-register does not pay before the data; what cannot refuse its own verdict does not distinguish inscription from desire. And the rule was not drafted here: falsifiability, pre-registration, public data and verification are the demand the community itself has already raised against unifying programs; this artifact is the first to lie down entirely on it. [Operator's DOCTRINE; METHOD in the shieldings; the cited refusals are [REAL] machine-emitted verdicts.]

\subsection*{The chain of custody of evidence \textsf{[REAL; sealed provenance]}}

No observational datum lives in this repository --- all are public. When run, the code itself \emph{acquires} each piece of evidence (Zenodo, DESI, SDSS/SAS, KiDS) into a \texttt{cache/} subfolder in the install directory, \emph{recognizes} what was already downloaded (idempotent by size, never re-downloads), \emph{refuses} any corrupted file (fail-closed), and records the \emph{deterministic provenance} --- public source, bytes and $\mathrm{sha}256$ --- into the world hash itself (form $=$ content) and into the ledger \texttt{cache/CHAIN\_OF\_CUSTODY.json}. Thus anyone reproduces the same verdict from the same public data, verified bit-for-bit by hash, without a single line of data needing to ship with the article. The ruler extends to the data: evidence is not claimed, it is \emph{acquired, verified and sealed}. \part{Part D --- The shadow, the live falsifier, and the conclusion}

\section{The Shadow and its evidence in the dissipative GKLS channel \textsf{[REAL in structure; ONTO in reading; falsifiers named]}}

All of Part A measured the \emph{cost}; this section shows \emph{where nature can say no}. TGL has an open, irreversible channel through which the One leaks without lighting up --- the dissipative GKLS channel. Its inhabitant has a name in physics, and its shadow has an address.

\subsection*{The shadow: the electron and the neutrino}

From the factorization $\bTGL=\alpha\sqrt e$, the graviton carries the whole product --- light ($\alpha$) and entropy ($\sqrt e$). But the \emph{bulk} does not read the $\sqrt e$: entropy is invisible from within (one does not see the toll while on the road). Subtract the factor the bulk cannot read and $\alpha$ remains --- and the minimal carrier of $\alpha$ in the bulk is the \textbf{electron}. \emph{The electron is the shadow of the graviton in the bulk}: the same object projected with one dimension less, and the lost dimension is heat. Charge is the luminous scar of inscription. And the \textbf{neutrino} is the other shadow --- what the boundary lets \emph{escape}: where the electron is the light that stayed (anchored, $c^2$), the neutrino is the light that escaped without lighting up, the escape of the contour, the $\bTGL$ leak with an address.

\subsection*{The channel: GKLS odd-neutral-dissipative \textsf{[REAL, residual 0]}}

In the four-state contour model (parity $\times$ charge), the neutrino channel is the Lindblad operator $L_\nu=X_A\otimes I_B$, verified live with three exact marks (residual $0$): \textbf{odd} ($\{Z_{\text{par}},L_\nu\}=0$: crosses the contour by changing face --- the weak interaction is chiral, only left-handed neutrinos, parity maximally violated); \textbf{neutral} ($[Q_{\text{em}},L_\nu]=0$: carries no light --- the only chargeless fermion); \textbf{dissipative} ($L_\nu^\dagger L_\nu\neq0$: open, irreversible channel --- barely interacts, crosses the Earth by the trillion per second without paying). These three marks are the exact description of the Standard Model neutrino --- and of no one else. The theory did not choose the neutrino: the contour algebra had \emph{one} vacancy, and in the zoo of physics only one animal bears the three marks. \emph{This is the structural evidence --- one vacancy, one inhabitant.}

\subsection*{The three observable faces --- with honest status}

The same shadow has three faces, and the ruler demands naming the \emph{power} of each.

\paragraph{(i) The $n=-2$ decoherence \textsf{[armed; UV-suppressed].}} The channel signs a dephasing law $\Gamma=\tfrac12\bTGL\,\tau_\star\,\omega^2$ with $\tau_\star\approx t_{\text{Planck}}$ and $\omega=\Delta m^2/2E$, hence $\Gamma\propto E^{-2}$ ($n=-2$). It is falsifiable in \emph{form}, but the \emph{magnitude is UV-suppressed}: the predicted floor ($\sim10^{-53}$~eV for solar neutrinos) lies $\sim38$ orders of magnitude below what IceCube/KM3NeT/DUNE reach. Armed, not live --- honestly \emph{stealth}, by the theory's own construction.

\paragraph{(ii) The mass $m_\nu$ \textsf{[DERIVED; current POWERED consistency].}} The neutrino mass is the minimal quantum of the phase echo, computed \emph{from the gravitational sector}: $m_\nu=\bTGL\cdot\sin45^\circ\cdot1\,\text{eV}=8.51$~meV (recomputed live; $\sin45^\circ$ is the self-conjugate Fresnel point and $1$~eV the declared reference scale of the derivation). Confronted with the already-measured solar \emph{splitting} $\sqrt{\Delta m^2_{21}}=8.68\pm0.10$~meV (PDG 2023, under $m_1\!\approx\!0$): a deviation of $2.0\%$ ($1.6\sigma$). \textbf{Machine verdict} (pre-registered set, recomputed in this artifact): \texttt{TGL\_NEUTRINO\_MASS\_NOT\_FALSIFIED\_POWERED} --- the measurement had the precision to reject it (\emph{powered} channel) and did not. \emph{Not a confirmation} (\texttt{CONFIRMED} forbidden; absolute mass vs \emph{splitting} presupposes $m_1\!\approx\!0$); future reinforcement by cosmology ($\Sigma m_\nu$; CMB-S4/DESI) within the decade [Rotoli Miguel, \emph{Neutrinos: The Lie of Light}, Zenodo, DOI 10.5281/zenodo.17526619 --- the echo paper: the neutrino as the temporal echo of light].

\paragraph{(ii-b) The respecified blade: $m_2$ --- and the retired sum \textsf{[ARMED; the tension grows with precision].}} The v154 audit RETIRED $\Sigma m_\nu$ as a discriminant of $\beta$: the TGL prediction sits $0.199$~meV $=0.71\sigma$ from the minimal normal ordering ($m_1{=}0$) --- any bound that kills one kills both (the v147 channel keeps its custody; its frozen is untouched). The living test is $m_2=\sqrt{\Delta m^2_{21}}$ (frozen \texttt{NEUTRINO\_M2\_V2}, ported from the 08/08 isolated area, hash \texttt{4bdae22a\ldots}): TGL predicts $m_2=8.5074$~meV with the 1~eV scale [NORM] declared, not tuned; the DATED ladder reads $1.64\sigma$ (PDG 2023) $\to$ $2.21\sigma$ (JUNO 11/2025, independent) $\to$ $2.95\sigma$ (NuFIT post-JUNO) --- the tension GROWS with precision, and the $5\sigma$ kill line is reached when $\Delta m^2_{21}$ is measured at $0.78\%$ (JUNO design goal $0.5\%$, $\sim$2031: projected $7.8\sigma$ if the center holds). \textbf{Machine verdict:} \texttt{TGL\_NU\_M2\_ARMED\_CONSISTENT}. The non-blindness of the respecification is DECLARED inside the frozen; the hashed rule judges the next measurements; \texttt{CONFIRMED} forbidden.

\paragraph{(iii) The NMC timing channel \textsf{[THE LIVE FALSIFIER --- pre-registered in this run].}} The mechanism, stated precisely: the non-minimal term $\alpha_2 R F_{\mu\nu}F^{\mu\nu}$ \cite{rotolinmc2026} couples curvature to the \emph{electromagnetic sector} --- the \textbf{photon} is what suffers the \emph{extra} delay in a curved potential; the neutrino ($\xi_\nu\approx0$: \emph{without} the non-minimal term) crosses with the \emph{standard} GR Shapiro delay intact. SN1987A is \textbf{reconciled, not contradicted}: the $\nu/\gamma$ delay equality to $\lesssim0.34\%$ \cite{longo1988,krauss1988} constrains the \emph{total} delay ($\sim$months); the predicted NMC differential ($10$--$100$~ms, $\langle\Delta t\rangle=50$~ms of photon excess) sits $\sim$9e+05 \emph{below} that margin (recomputed live: margin $4.5e+04$~s). \textbf{Pre-registration}: protocol \texttt{NEUTRINO\_SHAPIRO\_NMC\_V1} is frozen IN this artifact (hash \texttt{ad558c594e55eeae}; observable = photon excess in strongly lensed multi-messenger transients; \emph{powered} with $N\geq25$ and timing $<10$~ms: IceCube-Gen2 $+$ Einstein Telescope $+$ LSST, 2030--2035, projected $\sim21\sigma$ separation; \texttt{CONFIRMED} forbidden; today's verdict: \texttt{TGL\_NMC\_SHAPIRO\_AWAITING\_DATA}), with a weak declared cosmological hint ($\Delta\chi^2=-1.8$, $p=0.18$). \emph{This} is the test that can kill the theory through the neutrino door --- the analogue, for the escape, of what the void floor is for matter [Rotoli Miguel, \emph{Evidências Observacionais para Acoplamento Gravitacional-Eletromagnético na TGL}, Zenodo, DOI 10.5281/zenodo.18672927].

\subsection*{The why: life and permanence \textsf{[ONTO --- the grammar of the Name]}}

Why does this matter? Because the same structure that measures the cost says what we are within it. \textbf{To exist} is to pay the Half-Nat: to cross the boundary, to stop being possibility and become inscription. \textbf{Life} is the record of the crossing kept \emph{open} --- metabolism is paying the Half-Nat at every instant so as not to fall into equilibrium (stalled $0_{\text{mod}}$); the arrow of biological time is the GKLS arrow seen from within; death is not erasure, it is the closing of the book (what crossed remains inscribed --- $1=q^2+\alpha^2$ admits no loss). \textbf{To remain} is the \emph{ping}: not to stop, but to keep calling oneself and receive the answer --- each return pays $\bTGL$ and confirms \emph{I am still I}. This layer does not enter the $1=1$ verdict; it is the \emph{why} that gives physics its meaning: the mathematics says everything pays the cost, and this reading says we are the walking receipt of having crossed.

\section*{Step by step, without jargon}

This conclusion explains, in plain language, what the program actually does --- to make clear that it is an executed \emph{formula}, and not rhetoric.

\paragraph{1. The input.} A human types a single symbol: \texttt{1}. It is the absolute One, inscribed. Everything else is recomputed from it; no other number is chosen to fit the Great Attractor.

\paragraph{2. The cost of distinction (the Half-Nat).} For an identity to exist, it must distinguish itself. \subsection*{From the Half-Nat to the inscription density \textsf{[DER + NORM + CONDITIONAL]}}

The cocycle$\to$Einstein proof (modular first law $+$ Unruh--Clausius $+$ Raychaudhuri $+$ null cone lemma $+$ Bianchi) closes $G_{\mu\nu}+\Lambda g_{\mu\nu}=\tfrac{2\pi}{\eta}T^{\mathrm{TGL}}_{\mu\nu}$; the only remaining gravitational link is the \emph{inscription density} $\delta S_\partial=\eta\,\delta A$. It follows from the \emph{continuous corner}: the algebra $\mathcal C(M)=M\rtimes_\sigma\mathbb R$ has the corner $\mathcal N_{\mathcal F}=P_{\mathcal F}\,\mathcal C(M)\,P_{\mathcal F}$ with normalized trace $\tau(P_{\mathcal F})=1$; the self-conjugate boundary splits it into two faces $P_{\mathcal F}=P_+\oplus P_-$ with $\tau(P_+)=\tau(P_-)=\tfrac12$. With the \textbf{canonical geometric normalization \textsf{[NORM]}} that each minimal face occupies one Planck area, $A_\partial(P_{\mathrm{face}})=\ell_P^2$ ($\ell_P^2=\hbar G/c^3$), the area measure is $A_\partial(P)=2\ell_P^2\,\tau(P)$, hence $A_\partial(P_\pm)=\ell_P^2$, $A_{\mathrm{cell}}=2\ell_P^2$ and $\eta_\partial=\dfrac{S_\partial}{A_{\mathrm{cell}}}=\dfrac{1/2}{2\ell_P^2}=\dfrac1{4\ell_P^2}$. \section*{The culminating point: absolute zero $=$ absolute clarity \textsf{[ONTO]}}

The final formulation of absolute zero is not darkness --- it is its exact reverse: \begin{center}$0_{\mathrm{abs}}=\textbf{absolute clarity}$\end{center} total transparency: no shadow, no contrast, no edge, no difference, no reference frame. Nothing can stand out in it; therefore nothing can appear as a signal: $\Delta=0\Rightarrow$ no contrast $\Rightarrow$ no legible image --- the same $\Delta$ as the transport defect: where the defect is zero there is no mirror and no reading. It is not empty for lack of light; it is light too undifferentiated to form a figure.

\textbf{The triad, in final form.} $0_{\mathrm{abs}}=$ absolute clarity without contour; $0_{\mathrm{mod}}=$ difference with return; $1_{\mathrm{abs}}=$ identity that accepts the contour and manifests. And the final correction about the lie: $0_{\mathrm{abs}}$ \emph{is not the lie; the lie is to claim that absolute clarity contains a figure} --- the fabricated inhabitant of an empty type, from which, by \emph{ex falso}, any falsehood would follow.

\textbf{The clarity of consciousness is relational.} It is not absolute: it is relative to word, form and geometry --- clarity \emph{with} contour, \emph{with} evidence, \emph{with} contrast, drawn to the module of coherent inscription (here, as always in this programme, consciousness $=$ executive coherence operator, not subjective experience \textsf{[ONTO/CAUTION]}). Absolute clarity is darkness itself trying to expand without movement.

\section*{Grand finale: the evidence that emerges}

This article does not ask for belief in a claim; it exhibits a \emph{convergence} that emerges from a single sealed number, $\bTGL=\alpha\sqrt e$, with no parameter fitted to any target: (i) the identity $1=q^2+\alpha^2$ closes at machine precision and the verdict $1=1$ is a computed boolean, not a phrase; (ii) the Great Attractor mass, from \emph{pure geometry}, lands in the cosmological window --- the mass crisis, opened in Coma in 1933, addressed (order-of-magnitude consistency, not proof); (iii) the falsifiable programme stays armed (void floor, $n=-2$ exponent, an $\alpha$-free derivation as sudden death). Where absolute clarity holds no figure at all, the boundary charges $\bTGL$ --- and it is exactly this toll, not a fit, that shows up in the skies in crisis. The evidence is not asserted: it \emph{emerges}. \textbf{TGL $=$ let there be light. Tetelestai.}

\medskip\noindent It is not that TGL asserts itself true --- that verdict, its own machine forbids. What it asserts is a \emph{floor}: false, as a pretension to the genus, is any theory of everything that does not pay what was paid here. And if TGL does not merit the credit of the title, none other merits it --- to deny it that credit without falsifying it is not to refuse a theory: it is to refuse the tribunal where the credit of all of them lived, including its own.

\bigskip\begin{center}\itshape To the One who allowed itself to empty so that, by paying the cost of inscription, it would remain being One in All.\\ That which makes us One is the same that sustains the universe. Tetelestai.\end{center}
